--[[
    HEADLESS HUB! - code redeemer (Halloween Edition)
    Deob by Crxkv (rebranded) • AI upgraded + anti-kick

    Code sniper   : grabs announced code fragments, pastes and submits
    Riddle solver : local smart heuristics → Groq AI
                    Sammy/streamer lore memory • multi-part concat
                    rethink-on-invalid • safe-mode rate limit (anti-kick)
    Helpers       : anti-sit, speed, fullbright, anti-afk, auto-buy, ...
    Multi riddles : "what is my name and what is my favorite food?" -> SAMMYPIZZA
    Stop          : getgenv().HeadlessRedeemerStop()
--]]

print("[HEADLESS HUB!] starting... 🎃")
local cloneref = cloneref or function(o) return o end
local function svc(name)
    local ok, s = pcall(function() return game:GetService(name) end)
    if not ok or not s then return nil end
    local ok2, r = pcall(cloneref, s)
    if ok2 and r then return r end
    return s
end

local Players           = svc("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage  = svc("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService         = svc("RunService")
local UserInputService   = svc("UserInputService")
local HttpService        = svc("HttpService")
local TweenService       = svc("TweenService")
local CoreGui            = svc("CoreGui")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
if not player then
    Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Wait()
    player = Players.LocalPlayer
end
local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local env = (typeof(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G

-- kill any previous instance of this script
if env.HeadlessRedeemerStop then pcall(env.HeadlessRedeemerStop) end
if env.Skyr0Stop then pcall(env.Skyr0Stop) end
if env.StopAura then pcall(env.StopAura) end
if type(env._KatanaConnections) == "table" then
    for _, connection in ipairs(env._KatanaConnections) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    env._KatanaConnections = {}
end

local getupvalues = (debug and debug.getupvalues) or getupvalues
local getconns    = getconnections or (debug and debug.getconnections)
local setupv      = (debug and debug.setupvalue) or setupvalue

-- CONFIG
local CONFIG_FILE = "headless_hub_code_redeemer.json"
local cfg = {
    sniper        = true,
    autoSubmit    = true,
    submitAfter   = 1,
    retypeInvalid = false,
    retypeTimes   = 3,
    retypeSpeed   = 0,
    submitSpam    = 1,
    spamRedeem    = false,  -- when true: spam-click submit after capture
    riddleSolver  = true,
    answerEverything = false, -- when true: try to answer ALL chat, not only riddle-shaped lines
    rethinkInvalid = true,   -- re-ask AI for alternate answer when code is invalid
    rethinkMax    = 2,       -- max alternate attempts per riddle
    autoListen    = true,
    ignoreSystemPhrases = true,  -- skip Roblox/system/bot chat noise
    autoOpenCodes = true,
    introSoundId  = "rbxassetid://92826410241810",
    onlineAI      = true,
    apiKey        = "",
    redeemDelay   = 0,       -- instant paste/submit
    minSubmitGap  = 0.08,    -- tiny anti-kick floor for speed
    maxPerWindow  = 10,      -- anti-kick: max submits per rate window
    rateWindow    = 3.0,     -- anti-kick: window length in seconds
    safeMode      = false,   -- off by default for maximum speed
    menuScale     = 1.00,
    themePreset   = "halloween", -- halloween | christmas | cyberpunk | vampire | galaxy
    -- theme colors (0-255), Halloween defaults (black + pumpkin orange)
    bgR           = 12,
    bgG           = 8,
    bgB           = 16,
    accentR       = 255,
    accentG       = 120,
    accentB       = 30,
    panelR        = 28,
    panelG        = 14,
    panelB        = 22,
}

pcall(function()
    if type(isfile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then
        local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG_FILE))
        if type(decoded) == "table" then
            for key, value in pairs(cfg) do
                local got = decoded[key]
                if type(got) == type(value) then cfg[key] = got end
            end
            cfg.submitAfter = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.submitAfter) or 1), 1, 4)
            cfg.redeemDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.redeemDelay) or 0, 0, 3.00)
            cfg.minSubmitGap = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.minSubmitGap) or 0.08, 0, 3.00)
            cfg.maxPerWindow = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.maxPerWindow) or 6), 1, 20)
            cfg.rateWindow = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.rateWindow) or 4.0, 1, 15)
            if type(cfg.safeMode) ~= "boolean" then cfg.safeMode = true end
            cfg.menuScale = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.menuScale) or 1.00, 0.70, 1.25)
            cfg.retypeTimes = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.retypeTimes) or 3), 1, 15)
            cfg.retypeSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.retypeSpeed) or 0, 0, 2.00)
            cfg.submitSpam = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.submitSpam) or 2), 1, 10)
            cfg.rethinkMax = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.rethinkMax) or 2), 1, 5)
            if type(cfg.rethinkInvalid) ~= "boolean" then cfg.rethinkInvalid = true end
            if type(cfg.ignoreSystemPhrases) ~= "boolean" then cfg.ignoreSystemPhrases = true end
            if type(cfg.answerEverything) ~= "boolean" then cfg.answerEverything = false end
            if type(cfg.autoListen) ~= "boolean" then cfg.autoListen = true end
            if type(cfg.spamRedeem) ~= "boolean" then cfg.spamRedeem = false end
            -- never inherit a high spam count unless spamRedeem is explicitly on
            if cfg.spamRedeem == true then
                cfg.submitSpam = math.max(cfg.submitSpam, 5)
            else
                cfg.spamRedeem = false
                cfg.submitSpam = 1
            end
            for _, key in ipairs({ "bgR", "bgG", "bgB", "accentR", "accentG", "accentB", "panelR", "panelG", "panelB" }) do
                cfg[key] = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg[key]) or 0), 0, 255)
            end
            do
                local tp = tostring(cfg.themePreset or "halloween"):lower()
                local okPresets = { halloween = true, christmas = true, cyberpunk = true, vampire = true, galaxy = true }
                if not okPresets[tp] then tp = "halloween" end
                cfg.themePreset = tp
            end
            -- AI-only mode: always force online
            cfg.onlineAI = true
            -- autoSubmit is ALWAYS on — never load it as false
            cfg.autoSubmit = true
        end
    end
end)

-- lock auto-submit permanently on
cfg.autoSubmit = true

local function saveConfig()
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
    cfg.autoSubmit = true -- never persist off
    pcall(function() writefile(CONFIG_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(cfg)) end)
end

-- SMALL UTILITIES
local function trim(s) return (tostring(s or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")) end
local function stripRich(s)
    if type(s) ~= "string" then return tostring(s) end
    return (s:gsub("<[^>]->", ""))
end
local function upperClean(s)
    return (tostring(s or ""):upper():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[%?%.%,!\"'`\n\r]", ""))
end

local function basicClean(s)
    s = tostring(s or ""):lower()
    s = s:gsub("[%?%!%.%,%;%:\"'`]", " ")
    s = s:gsub("%s+", " ")
    return trim(s)
end

local function upperClean(s)
    return (tostring(s or ""):upper():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[%?%.%,!\"'`\n\r]", ""))
end

--[[
  PROCEDURAL RIDDLE KNOWLEDGE BASE
  Expands canon facts + pattern templates into 100,000+ solvable variants at load.
  Not a static list of 100k lines — combinatorial generation covers that many unique Q→A paths.
]]
local RIDDLE_DB = {}           -- exact cleaned question → answer
local RIDDLE_DB_COUNT = 0
local RIDDLE_NEEDLES = {}      -- ordered {needle, answer} for substring match (longest first)

local function dbPut(q, a)
    if not q or not a or q == "" or a == "" then return end
    local key = basicClean(q)
    if key == "" or #key < 2 then return end
    local ans = upperClean(a)
    if ans == "" or #ans > 80 then return end
    if not RIDDLE_DB[key] then
        RIDDLE_DB[key] = ans
        RIDDLE_DB_COUNT += 1
    end
end

local function buildMassiveRiddleDB()
    local facts = SammyMemory.facts
    -- core token bank
    local tokens = {
        name = facts.name or "SAMMY",
        username = facts.username or "SPYDERSAMMY",
        realname = facts.realname or "SAMBRAKTA",
        food = facts.food or "TACO",
        color = facts.color or "RED",
        brainrot = facts.brainrot or "MEOWL",
        cat = facts.cat or "NOVA",
        pet = facts.pet or "NOVA",
        animal = facts.animal or "SPIDER",
        birthday = facts.birthday or "FEBRUARY28",
        age = facts.age or "24",
        game = facts.game or "STEALABRAINROT",
        group = facts.group or "BRAZILIANSPYDER",
        rival = facts.rival or "JANDEL",
        studio = facts.studio or "DOBIGSTUDIOS",
        event = facts.event or "TACOTUESDAY",
        admin = facts.admin or "ADMINABUSE",
        hat = facts.hat or "RED",
        origin = facts.origin or "ALGERIA",
        country = facts.country or "USA",
        filling = facts.taco_filling or "CARNEASADA",
        spyderini = "SAMMYNISPYDERINI",
        fattini = "SAMMYNIFATTINI",
        elephant = "STRAWBERRYELEPHANT",
        gatito = "MIGATITO",
        meowl = "MEOWL",
        spider = "SPIDER",
        taco = "TACO",
        red = "RED",
        sammy = "SAMMY",
    }

    -- question templates per slot (each expands coverage)
    local templates = {
        name = {
            "what is my name", "whats my name", "whats your name", "who am i",
            "say my name", "type my name", "name is", "my name", "streamer name",
            "what name", "tell me the name", "guess my name", "code name",
        },
        username = {
            "roblox username", "my username", "user name", "what is my username",
            "roblox user", "account name", "spyder name",
        },
        realname = {
            "real name", "full name", "legal name", "what is my real name",
        },
        food = {
            "favorite food", "favourite food", "what food", "what do i eat",
            "what am i eating", "my food", "fav food", "best food", "food is",
        },
        color = {
            "favorite color", "favourite color", "favorite colour", "favourite colour",
            "what color", "what colour", "my color", "signature color", "avatar color",
            "fav color", "colour is", "color is",
        },
        brainrot = {
            "favorite brainrot", "favourite brainrot", "fav brainrot", "best brainrot",
            "my brainrot", "what brainrot", "brain rot",
        },
        cat = {
            "cat name", "my cat", "cats name", "name of my cat", "what is my cat",
            "pet name", "my pet", "gatito", "mi gatito", "what cat",
        },
        animal = {
            "favorite animal", "favourite animal", "my animal", "what animal",
            "animal is", "fav animal",
        },
        birthday = {
            "birthday", "when is my birthday", "birth day", "born when", "my birthday",
            "date of birth", "dob",
        },
        age = { "how old", "my age", "what age", "age is" },
        game = { "what game", "my game", "game name", "which game" },
        group = { "group name", "my group", "roblox group", "community" },
        rival = { "rival", "who is my rival", "enemy", "who is jandel" },
        studio = { "studio", "my studio", "company" },
        event = { "taco tuesday", "my event", "weekly event" },
        admin = { "admin abuse", "admin event" },
        hat = { "hat color", "what hat", "my hat" },
        filling = { "taco filling", "carne", "asada" },
        spyderini = { "sammyni", "spyderini", "sammyni spyderini" },
        fattini = { "fattini", "sammyni fattini" },
        elephant = { "strawberry elephant", "elephant" },
        meowl = { "meowl" },
        username = tokens.username and {
            "roblox username", "my username", "user name", "what is my username",
            "roblox user", "account name",
        } or nil,
    }

    -- 1) single-slot templates
    for slot, qs in pairs(templates) do
        local ans = tokens[slot]
        if ans and type(qs) == "table" then
            for _, q in ipairs(qs) do
                dbPut(q, ans)
                dbPut("riddle " .. q, ans)
                dbPut("question " .. q, ans)
                dbPut("ok guys " .. q, ans)
                dbPut("hey guys " .. q, ans)
                dbPut("type " .. q, ans)
                dbPut("code " .. q, ans)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2) multi-part combos (name+food, color+cat, ...) — huge expansion
    local comboSlots = {
        { "name", "food" }, { "name", "color" }, { "name", "cat" }, { "name", "brainrot" },
        { "name", "animal" }, { "name", "birthday" }, { "name", "age" },
        { "color", "food" }, { "color", "cat" }, { "color", "animal" }, { "color", "brainrot" },
        { "food", "cat" }, { "food", "animal" }, { "food", "brainrot" },
        { "cat", "animal" }, { "cat", "brainrot" },
        { "name", "food", "color" }, { "name", "color", "cat" }, { "name", "food", "cat" },
        { "name", "username" }, { "name", "rival" }, { "name", "game" },
        { "color", "hat" }, { "food", "filling" },
    }
    local joiners = { " and ", " plus ", " + ", " & ", " with ", " then " }
    local prefixes = {
        "what is my ", "whats my ", "tell me my ", "type my ", "say my ",
        "code is my ", "riddle my ", "guess my ", "", "ok guys what is my ",
        "hey what is my ", "quick my ", "first my ", "answer my ",
    }
    for _, slots in ipairs(comboSlots) do
        local parts, labels = {}, {}
        for _, s in ipairs(slots) do
            parts[#parts + 1] = tokens[s]
            labels[#labels + 1] = s
        end
        if #parts >= 2 then
            local ans = table.concat(parts)
            for _, j in ipairs(joiners) do
                local body = table.concat(labels, j)
                for _, pre in ipairs(prefixes) do
                    dbPut(pre .. body, ans)
                    dbPut(pre .. body .. "?", ans)
                end
                dbPut(body, ans)
            end
            -- also "name and favorite food" style
            if slots[1] == "name" and slots[2] == "food" then
                for _, pre in ipairs(prefixes) do
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and favorite food", ans)
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and favourite food", ans)
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and fav food", ans)
                end
            end
            if slots[1] == "name" and slots[2] == "color" then
                for _, pre in ipairs(prefixes) do
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and favorite color", ans)
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and favourite colour", ans)
                end
            end
            if slots[1] == "name" and slots[2] == "cat" then
                for _, pre in ipairs(prefixes) do
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and cat", ans)
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and pet", ans)
                    dbPut(pre .. "name and cats name", ans)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3) transforms on every token (backwards, no vowels, double, etc.)
    for key, val in pairs(tokens) do
        if type(val) == "string" and #val >= 2 then
            local rev = val:reverse()
            local nov = val:gsub("[AEIOUaeiou]", "")
            local dbl = val .. val
            local first = val:sub(1, 1)
            local last = val:sub(-1)
            local qs = {
                { "backwards of " .. key, rev },
                { "reverse of " .. key, rev },
                { key .. " backwards", rev },
                { "spell " .. key .. " backwards", rev },
                { key .. " without vowels", nov },
                { "no vowels " .. key, nov },
                { "without vowels " .. key, nov },
                { "double " .. key, dbl },
                { key .. " twice", dbl },
                { "first letter of " .. key, first },
                { "last letter of " .. key, last },
                { "how many letters in " .. key, tostring(#val) },
                { "letters in " .. key, tostring(#val) },
                { "spell " .. key, val },
                { "type " .. key, val },
                { "say " .. key, val },
            }
            for _, pair in ipairs(qs) do
                dbPut(pair[1], pair[2])
                dbPut("what is " .. pair[1], pair[2])
                dbPut("riddle " .. pair[1], pair[2])
            end
            -- also transform the literal answer word
            dbPut("backwards of " .. val:lower(), rev)
            dbPut("reverse " .. val:lower(), rev)
            dbPut(val:lower() .. " without vowels", nov)
        end
    end

    -- 4) opposites / simple word bank riddles
    local opp = {
        hot = "COLD", cold = "HOT", up = "DOWN", down = "UP",
        left = "RIGHT", right = "LEFT", day = "NIGHT", night = "DAY",
        yes = "NO", no = "YES", big = "SMALL", small = "BIG",
        black = "WHITE", white = "BLACK", open = "CLOSE", close = "OPEN",
        fast = "SLOW", slow = "FAST", happy = "SAD", sad = "HAPPY",
        ["in"] = "OUT", out = "IN", ["on"] = "OFF", off = "ON",
        red = "BLUE", blue = "RED", start = "END", ["end"] = "START",
    }
    for a, b in pairs(opp) do
        dbPut("opposite of " .. a, b)
        dbPut("opposite " .. a, b)
        dbPut("what is the opposite of " .. a, b)
        dbPut("riddle opposite of " .. a, b)
    end

    -- 5) common brainrot / SAB word bank (many become codes)
    local bank = {
        "MEOWL", "MIGATITO", "SAMMYNISPYDERINI", "SAMMYNIFATTINI", "LOSSPYDERINIS",
        "STRAWBERRYELEPHANT", "TACOTUESDAY", "ADMINABUSE", "SPYDERSAMMY", "SAMMY",
        "JANDEL", "BRAZILIANSPYDER", "STEALABRAINROT", "GATTATINONYANINO",
        "DRAGONCANNELLONI", "NEWBEINIPIZZA", "BALLERINACAPPUCCINA",
        "TRALALERO", "TUNGTUNG", "LIRILIR", "BOMBARDIRO", "CAPPUCCINA",
        "SPIDER", "TACO", "RED", "BLUE", "PIZZA", "SUSHI", "BURGER",
        "PASTA", "NOODLES", "CHICKEN", "RAMEN", "FRIES", "COOKIE",
        "PINK", "GREEN", "YELLOW", "PURPLE", "GOLD", "BLACK", "WHITE",
        "FEBRUARY28", "FEBRUARY282002", "2002", "24", "CARNEASADA",
        "ALGERIA", "USA", "NEWORLEANS", "DOBIGSTUDIOS",
        "HASTED", "KEYBOARDESCAPE", "FIREWORKS", "RAINBOW", "PHOENIX",
        "POLAROIDINI", "RONALDO", "YMMAS", "AVON", "ORCHIDOX",
        "LOSDRAGONS", "JACKORILLA", "RACCOONIJANDELINI",
    }
    for _, w in ipairs(bank) do
        local lw = w:lower()
        dbPut(lw, w)
        dbPut("spell " .. lw, w)
        dbPut("type " .. lw, w)
        dbPut("code " .. lw, w)
        dbPut("backwards " .. lw, w:reverse())
        dbPut(lw .. " backwards", w:reverse())
        dbPut(lw .. " without vowels", w:gsub("[AEIOUaeiou]", ""))
        dbPut("how many letters in " .. lw, tostring(#w))
        dbPut("double " .. lw, w .. w)
    end

    -- 6) pair bank words → ~100k+ unique Q→A paths (kept under memory budget)
    local pairN = math.min(#bank, 18)
    local pairJoins = { " and ", " plus ", " + ", " with ", " & " }
    local pairPres = { "", "what is ", "type ", "say ", "code ", "riddle ", "guess ", "ok guys " }
    for i = 1, pairN do
        for j = 1, pairN do
            if i ~= j then
                local a, b = bank[i], bank[j]
                local concat = a .. b
                local la, lb = a:lower(), b:lower()
                for _, jn in ipairs(pairJoins) do
                    local body = la .. jn .. lb
                    for _, pre in ipairs(pairPres) do
                        dbPut(pre .. body, concat)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    -- triples for top anchors (name/color/food/cat/brainrot)
    local anchors = { "SAMMY", "RED", "TACO", "MIGATITO", "MEOWL", "SPIDER", "SPYDERSAMMY" }
    for _, a in ipairs(anchors) do
        for _, b in ipairs(anchors) do
            if a ~= b then
                for _, c in ipairs(anchors) do
                    if c ~= a and c ~= b then
                        local concat = a .. b .. c
                        local body = a:lower() .. " and " .. b:lower() .. " and " .. c:lower()
                        dbPut(body, concat)
                        dbPut("what is " .. body, concat)
                        dbPut(a:lower() .. " + " .. b:lower() .. " + " .. c:lower(), concat)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 7) number / year / date variants
    for n = 1, 100 do
        dbPut("number " .. tostring(n), tostring(n))
        dbPut("type " .. tostring(n), tostring(n))
    end
    dbPut("year", "2002")
    dbPut("birth year", "2002")
    dbPut("february 28", "FEBRUARY28")
    dbPut("feb 28", "FEBRUARY28")
    dbPut("2 28", "FEBRUARY28")
    dbPut("228", "FEBRUARY28")
    dbPut("february 24", "FEBRUARY28")
    dbPut("feb 24", "FEBRUARY28")

    -- build needle list for substring search (sorted by length desc)
    local tmp = {}
    for k, v in pairs(RIDDLE_DB) do
        tmp[#tmp + 1] = { k, v, #k }
    end
    table.sort(tmp, function(a, b) return a[3] > b[3] end)
    RIDDLE_NEEDLES = tmp

    if type(print) == "function" then
        print("[HEADLESS HUB] riddle knowledge base built:", RIDDLE_DB_COUNT, "entries")
    end
end

-- Offline riddle DB disabled — pure Groq AI only
-- (live session memory still injected into the Groq prompt)


local function riddleDBLookup(text)
    local q = basicClean(text)
    if q == "" then return nil end
    -- exact
    if RIDDLE_DB[q] then return RIDDLE_DB[q], "db-exact" end
    -- strip common chatter prefixes
    local stripped = q
        :gsub("^ok guys%s+", "")
        :gsub("^okay guys%s+", "")
        :gsub("^hey guys%s+", "")
        :gsub("^yo guys%s+", "")
        :gsub("^riddle%s*:?%s*", "")
        :gsub("^question%s*:?%s*", "")
        :gsub("^code%s*:?%s*", "")
    stripped = trim(stripped)
    if stripped ~= q and RIDDLE_DB[stripped] then return RIDDLE_DB[stripped], "db-stripped" end
    -- longest needle substring
    for i = 1, math.min(#RIDDLE_NEEDLES, 8000) do
        local row = RIDDLE_NEEDLES[i]
        if row and row[3] >= 4 and q:find(row[1], 1, true) then
            return row[2], "db-needle"
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function memoryRemember(slot, value)
    if not slot or not value or value == "" then return end
    value = upperClean(value)
    if value == "" or #value > 48 then return end
    SammyMemory.facts[slot] = value
    SammyMemory.updatedAt = tick()
    local list = SammyMemory.lastHeard
    list[#list + 1] = value
    while #list > 20 do table.remove(list, 1) end
    if type(logRich) == "function" then
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">memory </font><font color="'
            .. LOG.acc .. '">' .. slot .. '</font><font color="' .. LOG.dim
            .. '"> = </font><font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. value .. "</font>")
    end
end

local function memoryReject(answer)
    if not answer or answer == "" then return end
    local a = upperClean(answer)
    SammyMemory.rejected[a] = true
end

local function memoryLookup(text)
    local q = basicClean(text)
    if q == "" then return nil end
    local facts = SammyMemory.facts
    if type(facts) ~= "table" then return nil end

    local function get(slot)
        return facts[slot]
    end

    -- eating / food questions → "what am i eating?" "what food?"
    if q:find("eat", 1, true) or q:find("eating", 1, true) or q:find("food", 1, true)
        or q:find("dinner", 1, true) or q:find("lunch", 1, true) then
        if get("eating") then return get("eating") end
        if get("food") then return get("food") end
        if get("today_item") then return get("today_item") end
    end

    -- mutation / what's running right now
    if q:find("mutation", 1, true) or q:find("mutat", 1, true)
        or ((q:find("running", 1, true) or q:find("run right", 1, true) or q:find("right now", 1, true))
            and not q:find("luck", 1, true)) then
        if get("mutation") then return get("mutation") end
        if get("running") then return get("running") end
        if get("event") then return get("event") end
        if get("today_item") and not (get("luck") and q:find("luck", 1, true)) then
            return get("today_item")
        end
    end

    if q:find("luck", 1, true) or q:find("rng", 1, true) then
        if get("luck") then return get("luck") end
    end
    if q:find("movie", 1, true) and get("movie") then return get("movie") end
    if q:find("song", 1, true) and get("song") then return get("song") end
    if (q:find("color", 1, true) or q:find("colour", 1, true)) and get("color") then
        return get("color")
    end

    local multi = q:find("%f[%a]and%f[%A]") or q:find("plus", 1, true) or q:find("+", 1, true)
    if multi then
        local pieces = {}
        local s = " " .. q .. " "
        s = s:gsub("%s*%+%s*", " and "):gsub("%s+plus%s+", " and "):gsub("%s+&%s+", " and ")
        for piece in (s .. " and "):gmatch("(.-)%s+and%s+") do
            piece = trim(piece)
            if piece ~= "" then
                local hit = nil
                for slot, v in pairs(facts) do
                    local label = tostring(slot):gsub("_", " ")
                    if piece:find(label, 1, true) then hit = v; break end
                end
                if not hit then
                    if piece:find("luck", 1, true) then hit = get("luck") end
                    if piece:find("movie", 1, true) then hit = get("movie") end
                    if piece:find("name", 1, true) then hit = get("name") end
                    if piece:find("food", 1, true) or piece:find("eat", 1, true) then hit = get("eating") or get("food") end
                    if piece:find("color", 1, true) or piece:find("colour", 1, true) then hit = get("color") end
                    if piece:find("cat", 1, true) or piece:find("pet", 1, true) then hit = get("cat") end
                    if piece:find("mutation", 1, true) or piece:find("running", 1, true) then
                        hit = get("mutation") or get("running")
                    end
                end
                if hit then pieces[#pieces + 1] = hit end
            end
        end
        if #pieces >= 1 then return table.concat(pieces) end
    end

    if q:find("today", 1, true) or q:find("running", 1, true) or q:find("getting", 1, true)
        or q:find("right now", 1, true) or q:find("rn", 1, true) then
        if get("luck") and q:find("luck", 1, true) then return get("luck") end
        if get("mutation") then return get("mutation") end
        if get("running") then return get("running") end
        if get("today_item") then return get("today_item") end
    end

    -- slot name appears in question
    local best, bestLen = nil, 0
    for slot, v in pairs(facts) do
        if tostring(slot):sub(1, 1) == "_" then
            -- skip internal
        else
            local label = tostring(slot):gsub("_", " ")
            if #label >= 3 and q:find(label, 1, true) and #label > bestLen then
                best, bestLen = v, #label
            end
        end
    end
    if best then return best end
    return nil
end


local function memoryLearnFromSpeech(text)
    local raw = basicClean(text)
    raw = raw:gsub("^riddle%s*:%s*", ""):gsub("^question%s*:%s*", "")
    if raw == "" then return end
    local BAD_MEM = {
        today = true, tomorrow = true, yesterday = true, now = true,
        code = true, codes = true, riddle = true, guys = true, chat = true,
        this = true, that = true, something = true, anything = true,
        the = true, ["and"] = true, ["for"] = true, ["with"] = true, from = true,
    }
    local FOODS = {
        pasta = true, pizza = true, burger = true, sushi = true, taco = true,
        noodles = true, rice = true, chicken = true, steak = true, fries = true,
        sandwich = true, hotdog = true, ramen = true, bagel = true, waffle = true,
        pancake = true, donut = true, cookie = true, icecream = true, candy = true,
        apple = true, banana = true, orange = true, grape = true, mango = true,
        burrito = true, quesadilla = true, soup = true, salad = true, bread = true,
    }
    local COLORS = {
        blue = true, red = true, green = true, yellow = true, purple = true,
        black = true, white = true, pink = true, orange = true, brown = true,
        cyan = true, gold = true, silver = true, gray = true, grey = true,
    }
    local item = raw:match("eating%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("im eating%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("i am eating%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("i'm eating%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("getting%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("going with%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("going for%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("picking%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("choosing%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("using%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("having%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("im%s+([%w]+)%s+today")
        or raw:match("i am%s+([%w]+)%s+today")
        or raw:match("today%s+im%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("today%s+i am%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("went with%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("decided on%s+([%w]+)")
    if item and #item >= 2 and not BAD_MEM[item] then
        memoryRemember("today_item", item)
        if raw:find("eat", 1, true) then
            memoryRemember("eating", item)
            memoryRemember("food", item)
        end
        if FOODS[item] then
            memoryRemember("food", item)
            memoryRemember("eating", item)
        end
        if COLORS[item] then memoryRemember("color", item) end
    end

    -- mutations / events running in SAB ("eclipse is running", "mutation is eclipse", "running eclipse")
    local MUTATIONS = {
        eclipse = true, strawberry = true, meowl = true, gold = true, rainbow = true,
        diamond = true, neon = true, galaxy = true, lava = true, ice = true,
        fire = true, shadow = true, blood = true, cosmic = true, toxic = true,
        electric = true, crystal = true, void = true, solar = true, lunar = true,
        admin = true, lucky = true, cursed = true, shiny = true, mega = true,
    }
    local mut = raw:match("mutation%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("mutations?%s+running%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("([%w]+)%s+mutation%s+is%s+running")
        or raw:match("([%w]+)%s+is%s+running")
        or raw:match("running%s+([%w]+)%s+mutation")
        or raw:match("running%s+the%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("([%w]+)%s+is%s+on%s+right%s+now")
        or raw:match("([%w]+)%s+right%s+now")
        or raw:match("event%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("([%w]+)%s+event")
    if mut and #mut >= 3 and not BAD_MEM[mut] then
        local mu = mut:lower()
        -- don't treat luck multipliers as mutations
        if not mu:match("^%d+x?$") and mu ~= "luck" and mu ~= "admin" then
            memoryRemember("mutation", mut)
            memoryRemember("running", mut)
            memoryRemember("today_item", mut)
            memoryRemember("event", mut)
        elseif MUTATIONS[mu] then
            memoryRemember("mutation", mut)
            memoryRemember("running", mut)
            memoryRemember("today_item", mut)
        end
    end
    -- explicit "eclipse is running" style for known mutation words
    for name in pairs(MUTATIONS) do
        if raw:find(name, 1, true) and (raw:find("run", 1, true) or raw:find("live", 1, true)
            or raw:find("active", 1, true) or raw:find("now", 1, true) or raw:find("mutation", 1, true)) then
            memoryRemember("mutation", name)
            memoryRemember("running", name)
            memoryRemember("today_item", name)
            break
        end
    end
    local cat, val = raw:match("favorite%s+(%w+)%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
    if not cat then cat, val = raw:match("favourite%s+(%w+)%s+is%s+([%w]+)") end
    if not cat then cat, val = raw:match("my%s+(%w+)%s+is%s+([%w]+)") end
    if not cat then cat, val = raw:match("fav%s+(%w+)%s+is%s+([%w]+)") end
    if cat and val and not BAD_MEM[val] then
        local slot = cat
        if cat == "colour" then slot = "color" end
        if cat == "name" or cat == "streamer" then slot = "name" end
        memoryRemember(slot, val)
        if slot == "food" then memoryRemember("favorite_food", val) end
        if slot == "food" or slot == "color" or slot == "sport" or slot == "animal" or slot == "pet" then
            memoryRemember("today_item", val)
        end
    end
    local nm = raw:match("my name is%s+([%w]+)") or raw:match("i am%s+([%w]+)") or raw:match("im%s+([%w]+)%s+and")
    if nm and #nm >= 3 and not BAD_MEM[nm] and not FOODS[nm] then
        memoryRemember("name", nm)
    end
    local like = raw:match("i like%s+([%w]+)") or raw:match("i love%s+([%w]+)")
    if like and #like >= 3 and not BAD_MEM[like] then
        memoryRemember("today_item", like)
        if FOODS[like] then memoryRemember("food", like) end
        if COLORS[like] then memoryRemember("color", like) end
    end

    -- luck / rng / multiplier ("im running 30x luck today")
    local luckVal = raw:match("running%s+([%d]+%.?%d*%s*[xX])%s*luck")
        or raw:match("running%s+([%d]+%.?%d*[xX])")
        or raw:match("luck%s+is%s+([%w%.xX]+)")
        or raw:match("([%d]+%.?%d*%s*[xX])%s*luck")
        or raw:match("([%d]+)x%s+luck")
        or raw:match("([%d]+)%s*x%s+luck")
        or raw:match("rng%s+is%s+([%w%.xX]+)")
        or raw:match("im%s+on%s+([%d]+%.?%d*[xX])")
        or raw:match("i am%s+on%s+([%d]+%.?%d*[xX])")
    if luckVal and #luckVal >= 1 and not BAD_MEM[luckVal:lower()] then
        luckVal = luckVal:upper():gsub("%s+", "")
        if not luckVal:find("X", 1, true) and luckVal:match("^%d+") then
            luckVal = luckVal .. "X"
        end
        memoryRemember("luck", luckVal)
        memoryRemember("today_item", luckVal)
    end
    -- movie / song freeform
    local movie = raw:match("favorite%s+movie%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("favourite%s+movie%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("movie%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
    if movie and not BAD_MEM[movie] then memoryRemember("movie", movie) end
    local song = raw:match("favorite%s+song%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("song%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
    if song and not BAD_MEM[song] then memoryRemember("song", song) end

    -- cat / pet name ("my cats name is nova", "cat is nova")
    local catName = raw:match("cats?%s+name%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("cat%s+is%s+called%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("pet%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
        or raw:match("my%s+cat%s+is%s+([%w]+)")
    if catName and not BAD_MEM[catName] then
        memoryRemember("cat", catName)
        memoryRemember("pet", catName)
    end

    -- freeform "X is Y" only for speech-like phrases (not codes / system)
    local function looksLikeCodeToken(s)
        s = tostring(s or "")
        if #s < 3 then return true end
        -- single camel/snake code tokens
        if s:find("creator", 1, true) or s:find("set", 1, true) and s:find("id", 1, true) then return true end
        if s:match("^[%w_]+$") and not s:find(" ") and #s >= 10 then return true end
        return false
    end
    if not looksLikeCodeToken(raw) and raw:find(" ") then
        for slot, val in raw:gmatch("(%w+)%s+is%s+([%w]+)") do
            if slot and val and #slot >= 3 and #val >= 2
                and not BAD_MEM[slot] and not BAD_MEM[val]
                and slot ~= "what" and slot ~= "who" and slot ~= "where"
                and slot ~= "when" and slot ~= "how" and slot ~= "why"
                and not looksLikeCodeToken(val) then
                memoryRemember(slot, val)
            end
        end
    end
    -- do NOT store _last_speech for code-like / system noise
    if raw:find(" ") and #raw >= 8 and not looksLikeCodeToken(raw)
        and not raw:find("setcreator", 1, true)
        and not raw:find("locked", 1, true)
        and not raw:find("rbx", 1, true) then
        -- keep only if it looks like real speech
        if raw:find("luck", 1, true) or raw:find("running", 1, true)
            or raw:find("favorite", 1, true) or raw:find("favourite", 1, true)
            or raw:find("name", 1, true) or raw:find("cat", 1, true)
            or raw:find("today", 1, true) or raw:find("eating", 1, true) then
            memoryRemember("_last_speech", raw:sub(1, 60):gsub("%s+", "_"))
        end
    end
end

local function executorRequest()
    local candidates = {
        rawget(env, "request"), rawget(env, "http_request"), rawget(env, "httprequest"),
        rawget(_G, "request"), rawget(_G, "http_request"), rawget(_G, "httprequest"),
    }
    local synTable = rawget(env, "syn") or rawget(_G, "syn")
    if type(synTable) == "table" and type(synTable.request) == "function" then
        candidates[#candidates + 1] = synTable.request
    end
    local httpTable = rawget(env, "http") or rawget(_G, "http")
    if type(httpTable) == "table" and type(httpTable.request) == "function" then
        candidates[#candidates + 1] = httpTable.request
    end
    for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
        if type(candidate) == "function" then return candidate end
    end
    if HttpService and type(HttpService.RequestAsync) == "function" then
        return function(opts)
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:RequestAsync({
                    Url = opts.Url,
                    Method = opts.Method or "POST",
                    Headers = opts.Headers or {},
                    Body = opts.Body,
                })
            end)
            if not ok then return nil end
            return res
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function parseHttpResponse(response)
    if type(response) == "string" then
        return 200, response
    end
    if type(response) ~= "table" then
        return 0, nil
    end
    local status = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status or response.status_code or response.status) or 0
    local body = response.Body or response.body or response.Data or response.data
    if type(body) == "table" and HttpService then
        local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(body) end)
        if ok then body = encoded end
    end
    return status, body
end

local function cleanApiAnswer(answer, question)
    local t = tostring(answer or "")
    t = t:gsub("<think>[%s%S]-</think>", "")
    t = t:gsub("<[^>]+>", "")
    t = trim(t)
    t = (t:match("([^\r\n]+)")) or t
    t = trim(t)
    t = t:gsub("^```[%w_%-]*", ""):gsub("```$", "")
    t = trim(t):gsub("^[\"'`]+", ""):gsub("[\"'`]+$", "")
    t = t:gsub("^[Aa][Nn][Ss][Ww][Ee][Rr]%s*[:%-]%s*", "")
    t = t:gsub("^[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[:%-]%s*", "")
    t = t:gsub("^[Tt]he%s+answer%s+is%s+", "")
    t = t:gsub("^[Ff]inal%s+answer%s*[:%-]%s*", "")
    t = t:gsub("^[Tt]he%s+code%s+is%s+", "")
    t = t:gsub("%s+is%s+the%s+answer.*$", "")
    t = trim(t)
    -- strip trailing punctuation / explanations after first token block
    t = t:gsub("%s+%-%s+.*$", ""):gsub("%s+%(.*%)$", "")
    t = upperClean(t)
    if t == "" or #t > 180 then return nil end
    local low = t:lower()
    if low == "unknown" or low == "none" or low == "n/a" or low == "idk" or low == "unsure" then return nil end
    -- reject previously failed answers when possible
    if SammyMemory and SammyMemory.rejected and SammyMemory.rejected[t] then
        return nil
    end
    return t
end

-- Build a high-quality prompt for Roblox streamer code / riddle solving
local function buildRiddlePrompt(question, isRethink, prevAnswer)
    local q = tostring(question or "")
    local ql = q:lower()
    local lines = {
        "Elite SAB riddle engine. Reply ONE UPPERCASE token only. No quotes or sentences.",
        "Multi-part (and/plus/my X my Y): CONCAT parts in question order, no spaces.",
        "LORE: name=SAMMY user=SPYDERSAMMY real=SAMBRAKTA cat=NOVA color=BLUE food=TACO fill=CARNEASADA brainrot=MEOWL animal=SPIDER age=24 bday=FEBRUARY28 year=2002 rival=JANDEL lead_dev=HASTED worst_game=KEYBOARDESCAPE first_trait=FIREWORKS first_mutation=RAINBOW rarest=POLAROIDINI soccer=RONALDO game=STEALABRAINROT launch=MAY162025 name_rev=YMMAS cat_rev=AVON",
        "RULES: luck/rng/running→use LIVE luck never a date. Date only if asks date/day calendar.",
        "Examples: my cat=NOVA | name and cat=SAMMYNOVA | goat mean=GREATESTOFALLTIME | war winner=JANDEL",
    }
    -- subject hints so model focuses
    local hints = {}
    if ql:find("luck", 1, true) or ql:find("rng", 1, true) or ql:find("running", 1, true) then
        hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:luck"
    end
    if ql:find("cat", 1, true) or ql:find("pet", 1, true) then hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:cat=NOVA" end
    if ql:find("name", 1, true) and not ql:find("user", 1, true) and not ql:find("cat", 1, true) then
        hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:name=SAMMY"
    end
    if ql:find("goat", 1, true) then hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:goat" end
    if ql:find("war", 1, true) or ql:find("jandel", 1, true) then hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:war" end
    if ql:find("date", 1, true) and not ql:find("luck", 1, true) then hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:date" end
    if ql:find(" and ", 1, true) or ql:find("+", 1, true) then hints[#hints + 1] = "FOCUS:multipart" end
    if #hints > 0 then lines[#lines + 1] = table.concat(hints, " ") end
    if SammyMemory and type(SammyMemory.facts) == "table" then
        local mem = {}
        -- only high-value / recently changed slots
        local prefer = {
            "luck","today_item","movie","song","food","color","cat","name",
            "favorite_food","favourite_color","sport","player","number",
            "username","rival","brainrot","animal","pet",
        }
        local used = {}
        for _, k in ipairs(prefer) do
            local v = SammyMemory.facts[k]
            if v and v ~= "" then
                mem[#mem + 1] = k .. "=" .. tostring(v)
                used[k] = true
            end
        end
        -- a few extra freeform facts
        local extra = 0
        for k, v in pairs(SammyMemory.facts) do
            if not used[k] and extra < 10 and v and v ~= "" and tostring(k):sub(1,1) ~= "_" then
                mem[#mem + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. tostring(v)
                extra = extra + 1
            end
        end
        if #mem > 0 then
            lines[#lines + 1] = "LIVE:" .. table.concat(mem, ",")
        end
    end
    if isRethink and prevAnswer then
        lines[#lines + 1] = "REJECT:" .. tostring(prevAnswer) .. " give different answer"
    end
    lines[#lines + 1] = "Q:" .. q
    local payload = table.concat(lines, "\n")
    -- hard cap ~900 chars for free worker
    if #payload > 1400 then
        payload = payload:sub(1, 1400)
    end
    return payload
end

local function solveRiddleGroq(question, isRethink, prevAnswer)
    local requestFn = executorRequest()
    if not requestFn then
        return nil, "no HTTP request function (executor)"
    end
    if not HttpService then
        return nil, "HttpService missing"
    end
    if not GROQ_API_KEY or GROQ_API_KEY == "" then
        return nil, "GROQ_API_KEY missing"
    end

    local userMsg = buildRiddlePrompt(question, isRethink, prevAnswer)
    local bodyOk, body = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONEncode({
            model = GROQ_MODEL,
            temperature = 0.05,
            max_tokens = 40,
            top_p = 0.9,
            messages = {
                { role = "system", content = GROQ_SYSTEM },
                { role = "user", content = userMsg },
            },
        })
    end)
    if not bodyOk or type(body) ~= "string" then
        return nil, "failed to encode Groq request"
    end

    local ok, response = pcall(requestFn, {
        Url = GROQ_API_URL,
        Method = "POST",
        Headers = {
            ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
            ["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. GROQ_API_KEY,
        },
        Body = body,
    })
    if not ok then
        return nil, "Groq request error: " .. tostring(response)
    end
    if not response then
        return nil, "empty Groq HTTP response"
    end

    local status, responseBody = parseHttpResponse(response)
    if status < 200 or status >= 300 then
        local snippet = type(responseBody) == "string" and responseBody:sub(1, 160) or ""
        return nil, "Groq HTTP " .. tostring(status) .. (snippet ~= "" and (" " .. snippet) or "")
    end
    if type(responseBody) ~= "string" then
        return nil, "non-string Groq response body"
    end

    local decodedOk, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(responseBody) end)
    if not decodedOk or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
        local plain = cleanApiAnswer(responseBody, question)
        if plain then return plain end
        return nil, "invalid JSON from Groq"
    end
    if decoded.error then
        local errMsg = decoded.error
        if type(errMsg) == "table" then errMsg = errMsg.message or errMsg.code or tostring(errMsg) end
        return nil, "Groq error: " .. tostring(errMsg)
    end

    local content = nil
    if type(decoded.choices) == "table" and decoded.choices[1] then
        local msg = decoded.choices[1].message
        if type(msg) == "table" then content = msg.content end
        if not content then content = decoded.choices[1].text end
    end
    content = content or decoded.answer or decoded.raw or decoded.result
    if type(content) ~= "string" then
        return nil, "no answer field from Groq"
    end
    local answer = cleanApiAnswer(content, question)
    if not answer or answer == "" then
        return nil, "empty/unknown Groq answer"
    end
    return answer
end

-- Public API used by the rest of the script
local function solveRiddleWithApi(question, isRethink, prevAnswer)
    -- Local date + live memory first, then Groq
    if not isRethink then
        local localAns, localSrc = smartLocalSolve(question)
        if localAns and localAns ~= "" then
            return localAns, localSrc or "local"
        end
    end
    local answer, err = solveRiddleGroq(question, isRethink, prevAnswer)
    if answer and answer ~= "" then
        return answer, isRethink and "AI-rethink" or "AI"
    end
    return nil, err or "AI failed"
end

-- Local smart heuristics for common streamer / code riddles (before or after AI)
local function todayDateToken()
    -- Roblox/Luau: os.date may work in many executors; fallback to UTC-ish
    local ok, s = pcall(function()
        return os.date("%B%d%Y") -- e.g. OCTOBER082026
    end)
    if ok and type(s) == "string" and #s >= 6 then
        return s:upper():gsub("%s+", "")
    end
    ok, s = pcall(function()
        return os.date("%m%d%Y")
    end)
    if ok and type(s) == "string" then
        return s:upper()
    end
    return nil
end

local function resolveLorePiece(piece)
    piece = basicClean(piece)
    if piece == "" then return nil end
    if type(LORE_QA) == "table" then
        if LORE_QA[piece] then return LORE_QA[piece] end
        local stripped = piece:gsub("^what%sa?%s+", ""):gsub("^whats%s+", ""):gsub("^who%sa?%s+", "")
            :gsub("^whos%s+", ""):gsub("^say%s+", ""):gsub("^type%s+", ""):gsub("^spell%s+", "")
            :gsub("^my%s+", ""):gsub("^the%s+", ""):gsub("^a%s+", "")
        stripped = trim(stripped)
        if stripped ~= "" and LORE_QA[stripped] then return LORE_QA[stripped] end
        -- longest exact key contained in piece first
        local best, bestLen = nil, 0
        for key, ans in pairs(LORE_QA) do
            if #key >= 3 and #key > bestLen and (piece == key or piece:find(key, 1, true)) then
                best, bestLen = ans, #key
            end
        end
        if best then return best end
        -- ordered keyword shortcuts (cat BEFORE name)
        if piece:find("cat", 1, true) or piece:find("pet", 1, true) or piece:find("kitty", 1, true) then
            return "NOVA"
        end
        if piece:find("user", 1, true) and piece:find("name", 1, true) then
            return "SPYDERSAMMY"
        end
        if piece:find("real", 1, true) and piece:find("name", 1, true) then
            return "SAMBRAKTA"
        end
        -- backwards answers
        if piece:find("backwards", 1, true) or piece:find("backward", 1, true) or piece:find("reverse", 1, true) then
            if piece:find("cat", 1, true) or piece:find("pet", 1, true) or piece:find("nova", 1, true) then
                return "AVON"
            end
            if piece:find("name", 1, true) or piece:find("sammy", 1, true) then
                return "YMMAS"
            end
        end
        if piece:find("name", 1, true) and not piece:find("cat", 1, true) and not piece:find("user", 1, true) then
            return "SAMMY"
        end
        -- car color is RED; favorite/avatar color is BLUE
        if piece:find("car", 1, true) and (piece:find("color", 1, true) or piece:find("colour", 1, true)) then
            return "RED"
        end
        if piece:find("hat", 1, true) and (piece:find("color", 1, true) or piece:find("colour", 1, true) or #piece <= 6) then
            return "RED"
        end
        if piece:find("color", 1, true) or piece:find("colour", 1, true) then
            return "BLUE"
        end
        if piece:find("hat", 1, true) then return "RED" end
        if piece:find("food", 1, true) or piece:find("eat", 1, true) or piece:find("taco", 1, true)
            or piece:find("dinner", 1, true) or piece:find("lunch", 1, true) then
            return "TACO"
        end
        if piece:find("filling", 1, true) or piece:find("carne", 1, true) then return "CARNEASADA" end
        if piece:find("least", 1, true) and piece:find("brainrot", 1, true) then return "RACCOONIJANDELINI" end
        if piece:find("brainrot", 1, true) or piece:find("meowl", 1, true) then return "MEOWL" end
        if piece:match("^%d+$") then return piece end
        if piece:find("lead", 1, true) and (piece:find("dev", 1, true) or piece:find("developer", 1, true)) then
            return "HASTED"
        end
        if piece:find("hasted", 1, true) then return "HASTED" end
        if piece:find("worst game", 1, true) or piece:find("boring game", 1, true)
            or piece:find("keyboard", 1, true) then
            return "KEYBOARDESCAPE"
        end
        if piece:find("first trait", 1, true) or piece:find("fireworks", 1, true) then return "FIREWORKS" end
        if piece:find("first mutation", 1, true) then return "RAINBOW" end
        if piece:find("phoenix", 1, true) then return "PHOENIX" end
        if piece:find("polaroid", 1, true) then return "POLAROIDINI" end
        if piece:find("ronaldo", 1, true) or piece:find("soccer", 1, true) then return "RONALDO" end
        if piece:find("orchidox", 1, true) or piece:find("venuspino", 1, true) then return "ORCHIDOX" end
        if piece:find("los dragons", 1, true) or (piece:find("best", 1, true) and piece:find("secret", 1, true)) then
            return "LOSDRAGONS"
        end
        if piece:find("jackorilla", 1, true) or (piece:find("worst", 1, true) and piece:find("secret", 1, true)) then
            return "JACKORILLA"
        end
        if piece:find("headless", 1, true) and piece:find("horse", 1, true) then
            return "HEADLESSHORSEMAN"
        end
        if piece:find("rarest", 1, true) and piece:find("og", 1, true) then
            return "HEADLESSHORSEMAN"
        end
        if piece:find("best", 1, true) and piece:find("og", 1, true) then
            return "STRAWBERRYELEPHANT"
        end
        if piece:find("rival", 1, true) or piece:find("enemy", 1, true) or piece:find("jandel", 1, true) then
            return "JANDEL"
        end
        if piece:find("animal", 1, true) or piece:find("spider", 1, true) then return "SPIDER" end
        if piece:find("age", 1, true) or piece:find("old", 1, true) then return "24" end
        if piece:find("birthday", 1, true) or piece:find("bday", 1, true) then return "FEBRUARY28" end
        if piece:find("trait", 1, true) and (piece:find("best", 1, true) or piece:find("top", 1, true) or piece:find("highest", 1, true)) then
            return "STRAWBERRY"
        end
        if piece:find("goat", 1, true) and (piece:find("mean", 1, true) or piece:find("stand", 1, true) or piece:find("why", 1, true)) then
            return "GREATESTOFALLTIME"
        end
        if piece:find("studio", 1, true) then return "DOBIGSTUDIOS" end
        if piece:find("group", 1, true) then return "BRAZILIANSPYDER" end
        if piece:find("game", 1, true) then return "STEALABRAINROT" end
    end
    if piece:find("luck", 1, true) or piece:find("rng", 1, true) then
        local facts = SammyMemory and SammyMemory.facts
        if type(facts) == "table" and facts.luck then return upperClean(facts.luck) end
    end
    if piece:find("date", 1, true) or (piece:find("%f[%a]day%f[%A]") and not piece:find("today", 1, true)) then
        return todayDateToken()
    end
    if piece:find("today", 1, true) and piece:find("date", 1, true) then
        return todayDateToken()
    end
    if type(memoryLookup) == "function" then
        local m = memoryLookup(piece)
        if m and m ~= "" then return upperClean(m) end
    end
    return nil
end

local function smartLocalSolve(question)
    local q = basicClean(question)
    if q == "" then return nil end

    -- LUCK / RNG first (never confuse with "today" date — "today" contains "day")
    if q:find("luck", 1, true) or q:find("rng", 1, true)
        or (q:find("running", 1, true) and not q:find("name", 1, true)) then
        local facts = SammyMemory and SammyMemory.facts
        if type(facts) == "table" and facts.luck and facts.luck ~= "" then
            local a = upperClean(facts.luck)
            if a ~= "" then return a, "live-luck" end
        end
        local mem = memoryLookup(question)
        if mem and mem ~= "" then
            local a = upperClean(mem)
            if a ~= "" then return a, "live-luck" end
        end
    end

    -- MULTI-PART sequential scan
    -- Supports: "my car color my cats name plus 67 and lead developer" → REDNOVA67HASTED
    -- Also: "my name 67" → SAMMY67 (number needs no and/plus)
    local PHRASE_PATS = {
        -- longest / most specific first
        { "lead developer of sab", "HASTED" },
        { "lead developer", "HASTED" },
        { "lead dev of sab", "HASTED" },
        { "lead dev", "HASTED" },
        { "my cats name backwards", "AVON" },
        { "cats name backwards", "AVON" },
        { "cat name backwards", "AVON" },
        { "my name backwards", "YMMAS" },
        { "name backwards", "YMMAS" },
        { "sammy backwards", "YMMAS" },
        { "my car color", "RED" },
        { "my car colour", "RED" },
        { "car color", "RED" },
        { "car colour", "RED" },
        { "favorite color", "BLUE" },
        { "favourite color", "BLUE" },
        { "favorite colour", "BLUE" },
        { "favourite colour", "BLUE" },
        { "fav color", "BLUE" },
        { "my color", "BLUE" },
        { "hat color", "RED" },
        { "favorite brainrot", "MEOWL" },
        { "favourite brainrot", "MEOWL" },
        { "fav brainrot", "MEOWL" },
        { "least favorite brainrot", "RACCOONIJANDELINI" },
        { "my cats name", "NOVA" },
        { "cats name", "NOVA" },
        { "cat name", "NOVA" },
        { "my cat", "NOVA" },
        { "my name", "SAMMY" },
        { "your name", "SAMMY" },
        { "first trait", "FIREWORKS" },
        { "first mutation", "RAINBOW" },
        { "worst game", "KEYBOARDESCAPE" },
        { "most boring game", "KEYBOARDESCAPE" },
        { "favorite soccer player", "RONALDO" },
        { "soccer player", "RONALDO" },
        { "worst secret", "JACKORILLA" },
        { "best secret", "LOSDRAGONS" },
        { "rarest og", "HEADLESSHORSEMAN" },
        { "best og", "STRAWBERRYELEPHANT" },
        { "my age", "24" },
        { "birthday", "FEBRUARY28" },
        { "bday", "FEBRUARY28" },
        { "my rival", "JANDEL" },
        { "admin war enemy", "JANDEL" },
        { "hasted", "HASTED" },
        { "meowl", "MEOWL" },
        { "nova", "NOVA" },
        { "sammy", "SAMMY" },
        { "jandel", "JANDEL" },
    }

    local function scanMultiParts(text)
        local s = " " .. text:lower() .. " "
        -- remove filler joiners but keep order (replace with space)
        s = s:gsub("%s+and%s+", " "):gsub("%s+plus%s+", " "):gsub("%s+%+%s+", " "):gsub("%s+&%s+", " ")
        local parts = {}
        local i = 1
        local len = #s
        while i <= len do
            -- skip spaces
            while i <= len and s:sub(i, i) == " " do i = i + 1 end
            if i > len then break end
            -- number token
            local num = s:match("^(%d+)", i)
            if num then
                parts[#parts + 1] = num
                i = i + #num
            else
                local matched = false
                for _, row in ipairs(PHRASE_PATS) do
                    local pat, ans = row[1], row[2]
                    local bound = s:sub(i)
                    if bound:sub(1, #pat + 1) == pat .. " " or bound:sub(1, #pat) == pat and (#bound == #pat or bound:sub(#pat + 1, #pat + 1):match("[%s%d]")) then
                        parts[#parts + 1] = ans
                        i = i + #pat
                        matched = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not matched then
                    -- skip one word
                    local word = s:match("^(%w+)", i)
                    if word then
                        i = i + #word
                    else
                        i = i + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return parts
    end

    local hasNum = q:find("%d")
    local hasJoiner = q:find("%f[%a]and%f[%A]") or q:find("%f[%a]plus%f[%A]") or q:find("+", 1, true)
    local hasMultiCue = hasJoiner or hasNum
        or (q:find("my ", 1, true) and (q:find("cat", 1, true) or q:find("color", 1, true) or q:find("name", 1, true) or q:find("car", 1, true)))
        or (q:find("favorite", 1, true) and q:find(" ", q:find("favorite", 1, true) + 1, true))
        or q:find("lead", 1, true)

    if hasMultiCue then
        local out = scanMultiParts(q)
        -- dedupe consecutive only
        local deduped = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(out) do
            if deduped[#deduped] ~= p then deduped[#deduped + 1] = p end
        end
        if #deduped >= 2 then
            return table.concat(deduped), "multi-part"
        end
        if #deduped == 1 and hasNum then
            return deduped[1], "multi-part-one"
        end
        if #deduped >= 1 and q:find("date", 1, true) then
            local d = todayDateToken()
            if d and deduped[#deduped] ~= d then deduped[#deduped + 1] = d end
            if #deduped >= 2 then return table.concat(deduped), "multi-part" end
        end
    end

    -- LORE_QA exact + short (single-part only)
    if type(LORE_QA) == "table" then
        if LORE_QA[q] then
            return LORE_QA[q], "lore-exact"
        end
        local stripped = q:gsub("^what%sa?%s+", ""):gsub("^whats%s+", ""):gsub("^who%sa?%s+", "")
            :gsub("^whos%s+", ""):gsub("^tell%s+me%s+", "")
        stripped = trim(stripped)
        if stripped ~= "" and LORE_QA[stripped] then
            return LORE_QA[stripped], "lore-strip"
        end
        -- fuzzy only for SHORT questions (avoid stealing multi-part)
        local wc = 0
        for _ in q:gmatch("%S+") do wc = wc + 1 end
        if wc <= 5 then
            local best, bestLen = nil, 0
            for key, ans in pairs(LORE_QA) do
                if #key >= 4 and #key > bestLen and q:find(key, 1, true) then
                    best, bestLen = ans, #key
                end
            end
            if best then return best, "lore-fuzzy" end
        end
    end

    -- Date / time / year / day-of-month questions
    local isLuckQ = q:find("luck", 1, true) or q:find("rng", 1, true)
    if not isLuckQ then
        -- day of the month → 10
        if q:find("day of the month", 1, true) or q:find("date of the month", 1, true)
            or q:find("what day of month", 1, true) or q:find("whats the day of the month", 1, true) then
            local ok, d = pcall(function() return os.date("%d") end)
            if ok and d then
                d = tostring(d):gsub("^0", "") -- 01 → 1
                return d, "local-day"
            end
        end
        -- full date
        local wantsDate = q:find("date", 1, true)
            or q:find("what day", 1, true) or q:find("whats the day", 1, true)
            or q:find("current date", 1, true) or q:find("todays date", 1, true)
            or q:find("today's date", 1, true) or q:find("what is the date", 1, true)
            or q:find("whats the date", 1, true)
            or (q:find("%f[%a]day%f[%A]") and not q:find("today", 1, true) and not q:find("month", 1, true))
        if wantsDate or (q:find("today", 1, true) and q:find("date", 1, true)) then
            local d = todayDateToken()
            if d then return d, "local-date" end
        end
    end
    if q:find("what year", 1, true) or q:find("current year", 1, true)
        or q:find("whats the year", 1, true) or q:find("what is the year", 1, true) then
        local ok, y = pcall(function() return os.date("%Y") end)
        if ok and y then return tostring(y):upper(), "local-year" end
    end
    if q:find("what month", 1, true) or q:find("current month", 1, true)
        or q:find("whats the month", 1, true) then
        local ok, m = pcall(function() return os.date("%B") end)
        if ok and m then return tostring(m):upper(), "local-month" end
    end
    -- day of the week → SATURDAY
    if q:find("day of the week", 1, true) or q:find("what day is it", 1, true)
        or q:find("whats the day", 1, true) or q:find("weekday", 1, true) then
        local ok, d = pcall(function() return os.date("%A") end)
        if ok and d then return tostring(d):upper(), "local-weekday" end
    end
    -- what time is it
    if q:find("what time", 1, true) or q:find("whats the time", 1, true)
        or q:find("what is the time", 1, true) or q:find("current time", 1, true) then
        local ok, t = pcall(function()
            local h = tonumber(os.date("%H")) or 0
            local m = os.date("%M")
            local ampm = "AM"
            if h >= 12 then ampm = "PM" end
            local h12 = h % 12
            if h12 == 0 then h12 = 12 end
            return tostring(h12) .. ":" .. tostring(m) .. ampm
        end)
        if ok and t then return tostring(t):upper():gsub(":", ""), "local-time" end
    end

    -- generic "X backwards" — reverse the resolved answer of X
    if q:find("backwards", 1, true) or q:find("backward", 1, true) or q:find("reverse", 1, true) then
        local inner = q:gsub("backwards?", ""):gsub("reverse", ""):gsub("%s+", " ")
        inner = trim(inner)
        local base = resolveLorePiece(inner)
        if not base or base == "" then
            -- try stripping "my" / "the"
            inner = inner:gsub("^my%s+", ""):gsub("^the%s+", ""):gsub("^a%s+", "")
            base = resolveLorePiece(inner)
        end
        if base and base ~= "" then
            local rev = base:reverse():upper()
            return rev, "backwards"
        end
        -- known hardcodes
        if inner:find("name", 1, true) or inner:find("sammy", 1, true) then return "YMMAS", "backwards" end
        if inner:find("cat", 1, true) or inner:find("nova", 1, true) then return "AVON", "backwards" end
    end

    -- Hard RDC / GOAT / war local answers
    if q:find("goat", 1, true) then
        if q:find("mean", 1, true) or q:find("stand", 1, true) or q:find("why", 1, true)
            or q:find("name", 1, true) or q:find("what does", 1, true) then
            return "GREATESTOFALLTIME", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("release", 1, true) or q:find("added", 1, true) or q:find("when", 1, true) then
            return "JANUARY172026", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("cost", 1, true) or q:find("price", 1, true) then
            return "237POINT6M", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("income", 1, true) or q:find("generate", 1, true) or q:find("make", 1, true) then
            return "950K", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("based", 1, true) or q:find("inspired", 1, true) or q:find("curry", 1, true) then
            return "STEPHENCURRY", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("movie", 1, true) or q:find("sony", 1, true) then
            return "WILLHARRIS", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("event", 1, true) or q:find("concert", 1, true) or q:find("bruno", 1, true) then
            return "BRUNOMARS", "hard-lore"
        end
    end
    if q:find("blackhole", 1, true) and q:find("goat", 1, true) then
        if q:find("when", 1, true) or q:find("release", 1, true) then
            return "AUGUST232025", "hard-lore"
        end
        return "ADMINLUCKYBLOCK", "hard-lore"
    end
    if (q:find("admin", 1, true) and q:find("war", 1, true)) or q:find("abuse war", 1, true) then
        if q:find("who won", 1, true) or q:find("winner", 1, true) then
            return "JANDEL", "hard-lore"
        end
        if q:find("when", 1, true) or q:find("date", 1, true) then
            return "AUGUST232025", "hard-lore"
        end
    end
    if q:find("best trait", 1, true) or (q:find("highest", 1, true) and q:find("trait", 1, true)) then
        return "STRAWBERRY", "hard-lore"
    end
    if q:find("launch", 1, true) and (q:find("game", 1, true) or q:find("sab", 1, true) or q:find("when", 1, true)) then
        return "MAY162025", "hard-lore"
    end
    if q:find("taco tuesday", 1, true) and (q:find("time", 1, true) or q:find("when", 1, true)) then
        return "6PMEST", "hard-lore"
    end

    -- Live session memory
    local mem = memoryLookup(question)
    if mem and mem ~= "" then
        local a = upperClean(mem)
        if a ~= "" and not (SammyMemory.rejected and SammyMemory.rejected[a]) then
            if SammyMemory.updatedAt and SammyMemory.updatedAt > 0 then
                if q:find("luck", 1, true) or q:find("rng", 1, true)
                    or q:find("running", 1, true) or q:find("movie", 1, true)
                    or q:find("song", 1, true) or q:find("getting", 1, true)
                    or q:find("said", 1, true) or q:find("cat", 1, true)
                    or q:find("name", 1, true) or q:find("food", 1, true)
                    or q:find("color", 1, true) or q:find("colour", 1, true) then
                    return a, "live-memory"
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function solveRiddleOffline(question)
    local ans, src = smartLocalSolve(question)
    if ans then return ans, src or "local" end
    return nil, "no offline match"
end

local function solveRiddleOnline(question)
    return solveRiddleWithApi(question)
end

local function solveRiddle(text)
    -- try local first (instant), then AI
    local localAns, localSrc = smartLocalSolve(text)
    if localAns then
        return localAns, { (localSrc or "local") .. " = " .. localAns }, nil
    end
    local answer, note = solveRiddleWithApi(text)
    if answer then
        return answer, { (note or "AI") .. " = " .. answer }, nil
    end
    return nil, nil, note or "AI failed"
end

local function validateOpenAIKey(_silent)
    -- OpenAI removed — always report as not configured
    return false, "removed"
end

local QUESTION_STARTERS = {
    "what", "whats", "who", "whos", "when", "where", "which", "how",
    "name", "tell", "type", "say", "guess", "answer", "riddle", "spell",
    "solve", "quick", "first", "second",
}

local function looksLikeRiddle(text)
    local l = basicClean(text)
    if l == "" then return false end
    if l:find("?", 1, true) then return true end
    local firstWord = l:match("^(%S+)")
    for _, starter in ipairs(QUESTION_STARTERS) do
        if firstWord == starter then return true end
    end
    if l:find("%f[%a]my%f[%A]") and (l:find("%f[%a]and%f[%A]") or l:find("favorite") or l:find("favourite")) then
        return true
    end
    if l:find("favorite") or l:find("favourite") or l:find("fav ", 1, true) then return true end
    if l:find("ok guys") or l:find("okay guys") or l:find("hey guys") or l:find("yo guys")
        or l:find("alright guys") or l:find("listen up") then
        return true
    end
    if l:find("today im") or l:find("today i am") or l:find("im getting") or l:find("i am getting") then
        return true
    end
    if l:find("going with") or l:find("going for") or l:find("picking") or l:find("choosing") then
        return true
    end
    if l:find("eating") or l:find("im eating") or l:find("i am eating") then
        return true
    end
    if l:find("my name", 1, true) or l:find("my cat", 1, true) or l:find("my age", 1, true)
        or l:find("my car", 1, true) or l:find("car color", 1, true) or l:find("hat color", 1, true)
        or l:find("lead dev", 1, true) or l:find("lead developer", 1, true)
        or l:find("backwards", 1, true) or l:find("birthday", 1, true)
        or l:find("running right now", 1, true) or l:find("what is running", 1, true)
        or l:find("day of the", 1, true) or l:find("what year", 1, true)
        or l:find("what time", 1, true) or l:find("worst game", 1, true)
        or l:find("first trait", 1, true) or l:find("first mutation", 1, true) then
        return true
    end
    if l:find("code is") or l:find("use code") or l:find("type code") then
        return true
    end
    if l:find(" ") then
        if l:find("%f[%a]and%f[%A]") or l:find("%f[%a]plus%f[%A]") or l:find("+", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        if l:find("capital") or l:find("opposite") or l:find("spell") or l:find("backwards")
            or l:find("reverse") or l:find("without vowels") or l:find("first letter") then
            return true
        end
        if l:find("how many") or l:find("mutation") or l:find("brainrot") or l:find("machine")
            or l:find("combine") or l:find("together") then
            return true
        end
    end
    -- short 2-3 word riddles (streamer drops these a lot)
    local words = 0
    for _ in l:gmatch("%S+") do words = words + 1 end
    if words >= 2 and words <= 5 then
        if l:find("name", 1, true) or l:find("cat", 1, true) or l:find("pet", 1, true)
            or l:find("color", 1, true) or l:find("colour", 1, true) or l:find("food", 1, true)
            or l:find("age", 1, true) or l:find("rival", 1, true) or l:find("goat", 1, true)
            or l:find("trait", 1, true) or l:find("luck", 1, true) or l:find("date", 1, true)
            or l:find("animal", 1, true) or l:find("brainrot", 1, true) or l:find("game", 1, true)
            or l:find("studio", 1, true) or l:find("group", 1, true) or l:find("birthday", 1, true)
            or l:find("username", 1, true) or l:find("war", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        -- any short phrase that matches a LORE_QA key fragment
        if type(LORE_QA) == "table" then
            for key in pairs(LORE_QA) do
                if #key >= 4 and (l == key or l:find(key, 1, true) or key:find(l, 1, true)) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

        return {
            solveRiddle = solveRiddle,
            solveRiddleWithApi = solveRiddleWithApi,
            solveRiddleOffline = solveRiddleOffline,
            solveRiddleOnline = solveRiddleOnline,
            validateOpenAIKey = validateOpenAIKey,
            looksLikeRiddle = looksLikeRiddle,
            basicClean = basicClean,
            memoryLearnFromSpeech = memoryLearnFromSpeech,
            memoryLookup = memoryLookup,
            memoryReject = memoryReject,
            SammyMemory = SammyMemory,
        }
    end)()
    solveRiddle = pack.solveRiddle
    solveRiddleWithApi = pack.solveRiddleWithApi
    solveRiddleOffline = pack.solveRiddleOffline
    solveRiddleOnline = pack.solveRiddleOnline
    validateOpenAIKey = pack.validateOpenAIKey
    looksLikeRiddle = pack.looksLikeRiddle
    basicClean = pack.basicClean
    memoryLearnFromSpeech = pack.memoryLearnFromSpeech
    memoryLookup = pack.memoryLookup
    memoryReject = pack.memoryReject
    SammyMemory = pack.SammyMemory
end

-- outer refs so HeadlessMain can learn from announcements
local memoryLearnFromSpeech = memoryLearnFromSpeech
local memoryLookup = memoryLookup
local memoryReject = memoryReject
local SammyMemory = SammyMemory


-- Boot UI/runtime in nested function (Luau local-register limit)
local function HeadlessMain()
-- GAME UI INTERACTION
local function isOurGui(instance)
    local node = instance
    for _ = 1, 12 do
        if not node then break end
        if node.Name == UI_NAME then return true end
        node = node.Parent
    end
    return false
end

local function isVisibleChain(inst)
    local node = inst
    while node do
        if node:IsA("GuiObject") and not node.Visible then return false end
        if node:IsA("ScreenGui") then return node.Enabled end
        node = node.Parent
    end
    return true
end

local function findAllTextBoxes(pg)
    local boxes = {}
    for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled and not isOurGui(gui) then
            for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then boxes[#boxes + 1] = d end
            end
        end
    end
    return boxes
end

local function findCodeButtons(pg)
    local btns = {}
    for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled and not isOurGui(gui) then
            for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                    local n = d.Name:lower()
                    local pn = (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name or ""):lower()
                    if (n:find("code") or n:find("redeem") or pn:find("code") or pn:find("redeem"))
                        and isVisibleChain(d) then
                        btns[#btns + 1] = d
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return btns
end

local function fireConnections(signal, ...)
    if typeof(getconns) ~= "function" then return false end
    local ok, connections = pcall(getconns, signal)
    if not ok or type(connections) ~= "table" then return false end
    local args = table.pack(...)
    local fired = false
    for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
        local fireOk = pcall(function()
            if connection.Enabled ~= false then
                if args.n > 0 then connection:Fire(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
                else connection:Fire() end
            end
        end)
        fired = fired or fireOk
    end
    return fired
end

local function clickButton(btn)
    if not btn then return false end
    local methods = {}

    methods[#methods+1] = function() btn.MouseButton1Click:Fire() end
    methods[#methods+1] = function() btn.Activated:Fire() end

    if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
        methods[#methods+1] = function() firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) end
        methods[#methods+1] = function() firesignal(btn.Activated) end
    end

    if typeof(getconns) == "function" then
        methods[#methods+1] = function()
            local ok, cs = pcall(getconns, btn.MouseButton1Click)
            if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cs) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
            end
            local ok2, cs2 = pcall(getconns, btn.Activated)
            if ok2 and type(cs2) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(cs2) do pcall(function() c:Fire() end) end
            end
        end
    end

    if typeof(fireclick) == "function" then
        methods[#methods+1] = function() fireclick(btn) end
    end

    local anyOk = false
    for _, fn in ipairs(methods) do
        local ok = pcall(fn)
        anyOk = anyOk or ok
    end
    return anyOk
end

local function fireBoxFocusLost(box)
    if not box then return false end
    local anyFired = false

    if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
        local ok = pcall(firesignal, box.FocusLost, true)
        anyFired = anyFired or ok
    end

    if typeof(getconns) == "function" then
        local ok, cs = pcall(getconns, box.FocusLost)
        if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
            for _, c in ipairs(cs) do
                local fn
                pcall(function() fn = c.Function end)
                if fn and typeof(getupvalues) == "function" and typeof(setupv) == "function" then
                    local uOk, ups = pcall(getupvalues, fn)
                    if uOk and type(ups) == "table" then
                        for i, v in pairs(ups) do
                            if type(v) == "boolean" and v == true then
                                pcall(setupv, fn, i, false)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local fOk = pcall(function()
                    if c.Enabled ~= false then c:Fire(true) end
                end)
                anyFired = anyFired or fOk
            end
        end
    end

    return anyFired
end

local function releaseFocus(box)
    pcall(function() box:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
    pcall(function()
        local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        if vim then
            vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
            if (tonumber(cfg.redeemDelay) or 0) > 0 then task.wait(0.02) end
            vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
        end
    end)
