    pcall(function() box:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
end

local RedeemCache = { box = nil, button = nil, frame = nil }

local function writeCodeToBox(box, code)
    if not box then return false end
    pcall(function()
        box.Text = code
    end)
    return box.Text == code
end

local function cacheStillValid()
    local box, frame = RedeemCache.box, RedeemCache.frame
    return box and box.Parent and frame and frame.Parent
end

local function currentCodeBox()
    if cacheStillValid() then return RedeemCache.box end

    local pg = playerGui or player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return nil end

    -- Exact CodeController path first: Codes.Codes.CodeRedeem.TextBox
    local codesGui = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
    if codesGui then
        local codesFrame = codesGui:FindFirstChild("Codes") or codesGui
        local redeem = codesFrame and codesFrame:FindFirstChild("CodeRedeem")
        local exact = redeem and redeem:FindFirstChild("TextBox")
        if exact and exact:IsA("TextBox") then
            RedeemCache.box = exact
            RedeemCache.frame = codesFrame
            return exact
        end

        -- Same fallback as the source build: first TextBox under Codes.
        for _, d in ipairs(codesGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then
                RedeemCache.box = d
                RedeemCache.frame = codesFrame
                return d
            end
        end
    end

    -- Last resort: any visible external TextBox.
    for _, box in ipairs(findAllTextBoxes(pg)) do
        if isVisibleChain(box) then return box end
    end
    return nil
end

local function findSubmitButton(box)
    if not box then return nil end
    local names = { "submit", "redeem", "claim", "confirm", "enter", "send", "apply", "ok", "use", "go", "check" }
    local node = box.Parent
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        if not node then break end
        for _, d in ipairs(node:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) and d ~= box then
                local n = d.Name:lower()
                local txt = ""
                pcall(function() txt = tostring(d.Text):lower() end)
                for _, key in ipairs(names) do
                    if (n:find(key) or txt:find(key)) and isVisibleChain(d) then return d end
                end
            end
        end
        node = node.Parent
    end
    return nil
end

-- OP REDEEMER SUBMIT / REDEEM LOGIC
-- Uses the same Codes.Codes-first path, button firing stack, FocusLost fallback,
-- visible TextBox fallback, and panel-opening fallback as op redeemer.
-- HEADLESS's redeemDelay + pending-result tracker are kept around the OP mechanism.
-- Anti-kick: rate-limit rapid redeems so the server doesn't trip spam / speed checks.
local _submitTimes = {}
local _lastSubmitAt = 0
local function waitAntiKick()
    if cfg.safeMode == false then return end
    local gap = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.minSubmitGap) or 0.35, 0, 3)
    local maxN = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.maxPerWindow) or 6), 1, 20)
    local win = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.rateWindow) or 4, 1, 15)
    local now = os.clock()
    -- enforce minimum gap between any two submits
    local since = now - _lastSubmitAt
    if _lastSubmitAt > 0 and since < gap then
        task.wait(gap - since)
        now = os.clock()
    end
    -- sliding window: drop old timestamps, wait if at cap
    local kept = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(_submitTimes) do
        if now - t < win then kept[#kept + 1] = t end
    end
    _submitTimes = kept
    if #_submitTimes >= maxN then
        local oldest = _submitTimes[1]
        local need = win - (now - oldest) + 0.05
        if need > 0 then
            if type(logRich) == "function" then
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.warn .. '">anti-kick: cooling ' .. string.format("%.2f", need) .. "s</font>")
            end
            task.wait(need)
            now = os.clock()
            local kept2 = {}
            for _, t in ipairs(_submitTimes) do
                if now - t < win then kept2[#kept2 + 1] = t end
            end
            _submitTimes = kept2
        end
    end
    _submitTimes[#_submitTimes + 1] = os.clock()
    _lastSubmitAt = os.clock()
end

typeAndSubmitCode = function(code)
    if not player then return false, "no LP" end
    local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return false, "no PlayerGui" end

    code = tostring(code or "")
    if code == "" then return false, "empty code" end

    -- throttle before any GUI write / remote fire
    waitAntiKick()

    local codesGui = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
    if codesGui then
        if codesGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            codesGui.Enabled = true
        end
        local codesFrame = codesGui:FindFirstChild("Codes") or codesGui
        if codesFrame then
            if codesFrame:IsA("GuiObject") then
                codesFrame.Visible = true
            end
            local cur = codesFrame
            while cur and cur ~= codesGui do
                if cur:IsA("GuiObject") then cur.Visible = true end
                cur = cur.Parent
            end

            local box = nil
            for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then
                    box = d
                    break
                end
            end

            local submitBtn = nil
            for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                    local n = d.Name:lower()
                    local txt = ""
                    pcall(function() txt = d.Text:lower() end)
                    if n:find("submit") or txt:find("submit") or n:find("redeem") or txt:find("redeem")
                        or n:find("claim") or txt:find("confirm") or n:find("enter") then
                        submitBtn = d
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if not submitBtn then
                for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                        local n = d.Name:lower()
                        if not n:find("close") and not n:find("x") and not n:find("toggle") then
                            submitBtn = d
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if box then
                if rememberPending then
                    -- don't wipe riddle flag if we already marked this code as riddle-sourced
                    if not (_pendingFromRiddle and _pendingText == code) then
                        rememberPending(box, code, true, false)
                    end
                end
                writeCodeToBox(box, code)
                do local d = tonumber(cfg.redeemDelay) or 0; if d > 0 then task.wait(d) end end
                -- ONLY multi-click when SPAM REDEEM is on; otherwise exactly 1 submit
                local spam = 1
                if cfg.spamRedeem == true then
                    spam = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.submitSpam) or 8), 2, 12)
                end
                local spamGap = cfg.spamRedeem and 0.04 or 0
                for i = 1, spam do
                    writeCodeToBox(box, code)
                    if submitBtn then
                        clickButton(submitBtn)
                    else
                        -- try any visible button named submit nearby
                        pcall(function()
                            for _, d in ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
                                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
                                    local n = (d.Name .. " " .. (d.Text or "")):lower()
                                    if n:find("submit") or n:find("redeem") or n:find("claim") then
                                        clickButton(d)
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    fireBoxFocusLost(box)
                    if spam > 1 then task.wait(spamGap) end
                end
                return true, "submitted via PlayerGui.Codes.Codes x" .. tostring(spam)
            end
        end
    end

    local function tryOpenPanel()
        local btns = findCodeButtons(pg)
        for _, btn in ipairs(btns) do
            clickButton(btn)
            if (tonumber(cfg.redeemDelay) or 0) > 0 then task.wait(0.03) end
        end
        return #btns > 0
    end

    tryOpenPanel()
    -- minimal open wait for speed
    task.wait((tonumber(cfg.redeemDelay) or 0) > 0 and 0.06 or 0.02)

    local box = nil
    local deadline = tick() + 0.75
    while tick() < deadline do
        local allBoxes = findAllTextBoxes(pg)
        for _, d in ipairs(allBoxes) do
            if isVisibleChain(d) then
                local n = d.Name:lower()
                local pn = (d.Parent and d.Parent.Name or ""):lower()
                if n:find("code") or pn:find("code") or n:find("redeem") or pn:find("redeem")
                    or n:find("input") or pn:find("input") or n:find("textbox") or n:find("enter") then
                    box = d
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not box then
            for _, d in ipairs(allBoxes) do
                if isVisibleChain(d) then box = d; break end
            end
        end
        if box then break end
        task.wait(0.03)
    end

    if not box then return false, "no codebox visible" end

    if rememberPending then
        if not (_pendingFromRiddle and _pendingText == code) then
            rememberPending(box, code, true, false)
        end
    end
    writeCodeToBox(box, code)
    do local d = tonumber(cfg.redeemDelay) or 0; if d > 0 then task.wait(d) end end

    local redeemBtn = nil
    local searchNames = {"submit","redeem","claim","confirm","enter","send","apply","ok","use","go","check"}
    local p = box.Parent
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        if not p then break end
        for _, d in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) and d ~= box then
                local n = d.Name:lower()
                local txt = ""
                pcall(function() txt = d.Text:lower() end)
                for _, sn in ipairs(searchNames) do
                    if n:find(sn) or txt:find(sn) then
                        if isVisibleChain(d) then
                            redeemBtn = d
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                if redeemBtn then break end
            end
        end
        if redeemBtn then break end
        p = p.Parent
    end

    local spam = 1
    if cfg.spamRedeem == true then
        spam = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.submitSpam) or 8), 2, 12)
    end
    local spamGap = cfg.spamRedeem and 0.04 or 0
    for i = 1, spam do
        writeCodeToBox(box, code)
        if redeemBtn then clickButton(redeemBtn) end
        fireBoxFocusLost(box)
        if spam > 1 then task.wait(spamGap) end
    end
    return true, "fallback methods used x" .. tostring(spam)
end

-- AA HELPER LOGIC
-- Auto Buy + Anchor are adapted from Blacan Auto Buy.
-- Anti Ragdoll is adapted from the supplied unpatched anti-ragdoll script.
local helperAutoBuyActive = false
local helperAnchored = false
local helperAntiRagdollActive = false
local helperAutoBuyElapsed = 0
local helperAntiRagdollCooldown = 0
local helperAntiRagdollConn

local function triggerWorkspacePrompts()
    for _, prompt in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            pcall(function()
                prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                prompt:InputHoldEnd()
            end)
        end
    end
end

local helperAutoBuyConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, deltaTime)
    if not helperAutoBuyActive then return end
    helperAutoBuyElapsed += tonumber(deltaTime) or 0
    if helperAutoBuyElapsed < 0.12 then return end
    helperAutoBuyElapsed = 0
    triggerWorkspacePrompts()
end)

local function setCharacterAnchored(state, character)
    local char = character or player.Character
    if not char then return end
    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function() part.Anchored = state end)
        end
    end
end

local helperCharacterConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    if helperAnchored then
        task.defer(function()
            character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            setCharacterAnchored(true, character)
        end)
    end
end)

local function forceAntiRagdollReset()
    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or not root or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

    pcall(function()
        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        for _, object in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if object:IsA("Motor6D") then object.Enabled = true end
            if object:IsA("Constraint") then object.Enabled = true end
        end
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid end
        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        humanoid.Sit = false
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local playerModule = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            and player.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        local controlModule = playerModule and playerModule:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")
        if controlModule then
            local controls = require(controlModule)
            if controls and controls.Enable then controls:Enable() end
        end
    end)
end

local function setHelperAutoBuy(state)
    helperAutoBuyActive = state == true
    helperAutoBuyElapsed = 0
    if helperAutoBuyActive then task.spawn(triggerWorkspacePrompts) end
end

local function setHelperAnchor(state)
    helperAnchored = state == true
    setCharacterAnchored(helperAnchored)
end

local function setHelperAntiRagdoll(state)
    helperAntiRagdollActive = state == true
    if not helperAntiRagdollActive then
        if helperAntiRagdollConn then helperAntiRagdollConn:Disconnect() end
        helperAntiRagdollConn = nil
        return
    end
    if helperAntiRagdollConn then return end
    helperAntiRagdollConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not helperAntiRagdollActive then return end
        local character = player.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        local stateNow = humanoid:GetState()
        local ragdolled = stateNow == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or stateNow == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or stateNow == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        if ragdolled and tick() - helperAntiRagdollCooldown > 0.15 then
            helperAntiRagdollCooldown = tick()
            forceAntiRagdollReset()
        end
    end)
end

-- EXTRA HELPERS (table state to stay under Luau local-register limit)
local HX = {
    speed = false, infJump = false, noclip = false, fullbright = false, antiAfk = false,
    fly = false, antiSit = false,
    speedValue = 50, defaultSpeed = 16, flySpeed = 50,
    speedConn = nil, infJumpConn = nil, noclipConn = nil, fullbrightConn = nil, antiAfkConn = nil,
    flyConn = nil, antiSitConn = nil, flyBV = nil, flyBG = nil,
}

local function hxHumanoid(character)
    character = character or player.Character
    return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function hxApplySpeed(character)
    local hum = hxHumanoid(character)
    if not hum then return end
    pcall(function()
        hum.WalkSpeed = HX.speed and HX.speedValue or HX.defaultSpeed
    end)
end

local function setHelperSpeed(state)
    HX.speed = state == true
    hxApplySpeed(player.Character)
    if HX.speed then
        if HX.speedConn then return end
        HX.speedConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not HX.speed then return end
            local hum = hxHumanoid()
            if hum and math.abs((hum.WalkSpeed or 0) - HX.speedValue) > 0.5 then
                pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = HX.speedValue end)
            end
        end)
    else
        if HX.speedConn then pcall(function() HX.speedConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.speedConn = nil
        hxApplySpeed(player.Character)
    end
end

local function setHelperInfJump(state)
    HX.infJump = state == true
    if not HX.infJump then
        if HX.infJumpConn then pcall(function() HX.infJumpConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.infJumpConn = nil
        return
    end
    if HX.infJumpConn then return end
    HX.infJumpConn = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if not HX.infJump then return end
        local hum = hxHumanoid()
        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)
        end
    end)
end

local function setNoclipParts(character, on)
    character = character or player.Character
    if not character then return end
    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function() part.CanCollide = not on end)
        end
    end
end

local function setHelperNoclip(state)
    HX.noclip = state == true
    setNoclipParts(player.Character, HX.noclip)
    if not HX.noclip then
        if HX.noclipConn then pcall(function() HX.noclipConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.noclipConn = nil
        setNoclipParts(player.Character, false)
        return
    end
    if HX.noclipConn then return end
    HX.noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if HX.noclip then setNoclipParts(player.Character, true) end
    end)
end

local function setHelperFullbright(state)
    HX.fullbright = state == true
    if not HX.fullbright then
        if HX.fullbrightConn then pcall(function() HX.fullbrightConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.fullbrightConn = nil
        pcall(function()
            local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
            lighting.Brightness = 1
            lighting.ClockTime = 14
            lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        end)
        return
    end
    if HX.fullbrightConn then return end
    HX.fullbrightConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not HX.fullbright then return end
        pcall(function()
            local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
            lighting.Brightness = 2
            lighting.ClockTime = 12
            lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
            lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
        end)
    end)
end

local function setHelperAntiAfk(state)
    HX.antiAfk = state == true
    if not HX.antiAfk then
        if HX.antiAfkConn then pcall(function() HX.antiAfkConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.antiAfkConn = nil
        return
    end
    if HX.antiAfkConn then return end
    pcall(function()
        player.Idled:Connect(function()
            if not HX.antiAfk then return end
            local vu = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
            vu:CaptureController()
            vu:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        end)
    end)
    HX.antiAfkConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() end)
end

local function setHelperFly(state)
    HX.fly = state == true
    if not HX.fly then
        if HX.flyConn then pcall(function() HX.flyConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.flyConn = nil
        pcall(function()
            if HX.flyBV then HX.flyBV:Destroy() end
            if HX.flyBG then HX.flyBG:Destroy() end
        end)
        HX.flyBV, HX.flyBG = nil, nil
        local hum = hxHumanoid()
        if hum then pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = false end) end
        return
    end
    if HX.flyConn then return end
    HX.flyConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not HX.fly then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = hxHumanoid(char)
        if not root or not hum then return end
        pcall(function()
            if not HX.flyBV or not HX.flyBV.Parent then
                local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                bv.Parent = root
                HX.flyBV = bv
            end
            if not HX.flyBG or not HX.flyBG.Parent then
                local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
                bg.P = 9e4
                bg.Parent = root
                HX.flyBG = bg
            end
            hum.PlatformStand = true
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local dir = Vector3.zero
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir = dir + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir = dir - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir = dir - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir = dir + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir = dir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
                dir = dir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
            end
            local spd = HX.flySpeed or 50
            if dir.Magnitude > 0 then
                HX.flyBV.Velocity = dir.Unit * spd
            else
                HX.flyBV.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            HX.flyBG.CFrame = cam.CFrame
        end)
    end)
end

local function setHelperAntiSit(state)
    HX.antiSit = state == true
    if not HX.antiSit then
        if HX.antiSitConn then pcall(function() HX.antiSitConn:Disconnect() end) end
        HX.antiSitConn = nil
        return
    end
    if HX.antiSitConn then return end
    HX.antiSitConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not HX.antiSit then return end
        local hum = hxHumanoid()
        if hum and hum.Sit then
            pcall(function()
                hum.Sit = false
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function helperResetCharacter()
    pcall(function()
        local char = player.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
    end)
end

local function helperRejoin()
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, player)
    end)
end

player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    task.defer(function()
        character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if HX.speed then hxApplySpeed(character) end
        if HX.noclip then setNoclipParts(character, true) end
        if helperAnchored then setCharacterAnchored(true, character) end
        if HX.fly then
            setHelperFly(false)
            setHelperFly(true)
        end
    end)
end)

local function applyPresetColors(name)
    local p = THEME_PRESETS[name] or THEME_PRESETS.halloween
    cfg.themePreset = name
    cfg.bgR, cfg.bgG, cfg.bgB = p.bg[1], p.bg[2], p.bg[3]
    cfg.panelR, cfg.panelG, cfg.panelB = p.panel[1], p.panel[2], p.panel[3]
    cfg.accentR, cfg.accentG, cfg.accentB = p.accent[1], p.accent[2], p.accent[3]
end


-- primitives (identical to the hub's)
local function new(class, props, parent)
    local inst = Instance.new(class)
    for key, value in pairs(props or {}) do inst[key] = value end
    if parent then inst.Parent = parent end
    return inst
end
local function corner(o, r)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r); c.Parent = o; return c
end
local function stroke(o, col, th, tr)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = col or Theme.Stroke; s.Thickness = th or 1; s.Transparency = tr or 0
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; s.Parent = o
    return s
end
local function tw(o, p, t, style, dir)
    if not o then return end
    local info = TweenInfo.new(
        t or 0.22,
        style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
        dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out
    )
    local anim = TweenService:Create(o, info, p)
    anim:Play()
    return anim
end
local function tween(o, t, p) return tw(o, p, t) end
local function twBounce(o, p, t)
    return tw(o, p, t or 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end
local function twSoft(o, p, t)
    return tw(o, p, t or 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Exponential, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end
local function addOutline(f)
    local o = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    o.Color = Theme.Accent
    o.Thickness = 1.5
    o.Transparency = 0.35
    o.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    o.Parent = f
    return o
end
-- frosted glass depth + theme-aware sheen
local function decoratePanel(f)
    local p = THEME_PRESETS[cfg.themePreset or "halloween"] or THEME_PRESETS.halloween
    local c0 = (p.panelGradient and p.panelGradient[1]) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local c1 = (p.panelGradient and p.panelGradient[2]) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 100, 60)
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient", f)
    g.Name = "ThemePanelGradient"
    g.Rotation = 128
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new{
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, c0),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c1),
    }
    g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.92),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.78),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.88),
    }
    local hl = Instance.new("Frame")
    hl.Name = "ThemeTopSheen"
    hl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 2)
    hl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 2)
    hl.BackgroundColor3 = p.particle or Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 100)
    hl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
    hl.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hl.ZIndex = 6
    hl.Active = false
    hl.Parent = f
    local hlg = Instance.new("UIGradient", hl)
    hlg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    }
    return g
end
local function label(parent, text, size, color, font, align)
    return new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextSize = size or 11,
        TextColor3 = color or Theme.Text, Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
        TextXAlignment = align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
    }, parent)
end

-- click helper that behaves on both mouse and touch
local function bindClick(button, callback)
    local last = 0
    local function fire()
        if tick() - last < 0.16 then return end
        last = tick()
        callback()
    end
    button.Activated:Connect(fire)
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(fire)
    button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then fire() end
    end)
end

    for _, name in ipairs({ UI_NAME, "KatanaHub", "HiddenUI", "skyr0wtf_CodeRedeemer", "Skyr0WtfUI", "ACECodeSniperUI", "AutoTypeCodesUI", "ACEPaste", "GuiznxRiddle" }) do
        local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild(name)
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local oldCore = CoreGui and CoreGui:FindFirstChild(name)
        if oldCore then oldCore:Destroy() end
    end
end)

            )
        end
        if UI.LauncherLogoFrame then

            UI.LauncherLogoFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(26 * s), math.floor(26 * s))

        end
        if UI.LauncherShadow then
            UI.LauncherShadow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86 + 0.06 * math.sin(t * 1.8)
        end
        task.wait(0.04)
    end
end)

local viewportConn
local function recalculateScale()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local h = camera.ViewportSize.Y
    local scale
    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
        scale = math.clamp(h / 1000, 0.52, 0.78)
    else
        scale = math.clamp(h / 800, 0.70, 1.0)
    end
    GlobalScale.Scale = scale
    Master.Size = UDim2.new(1 / scale, 0, 1 / scale, 0)
end
local function setupCameraListener()
    if viewportConn then pcall(function() viewportConn:Disconnect() end) end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        viewportConn = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(recalculateScale)
    end
    recalculateScale()
end
workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(setupCameraListener)
task.spawn(setupCameraListener)

-- Apply visual decor for a named preset (colors applied separately)
local function applyDecorTheme(presetName)
    local p = THEME_PRESETS[presetName] or THEME_PRESETS.halloween
    local mode = p.decorMode or "halloween"

    -- live background gradient from preset
    if UI.BackgroundImage then
        local g = UI.BackgroundImage:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if g and p.gradient then
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, p.gradient[1]),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, p.gradient[2]),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, p.gradient[3]),
            }
            g.Rotation = (mode == "cyberpunk" and 90) or (mode == "galaxy" and 200) or 145
        end
        UI.BackgroundImage.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(p.bg[1], p.bg[2], p.bg[3])
    end
    if UI.BackgroundShade then
        UI.BackgroundShade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(p.bg[1], p.bg[2], p.bg[3])
    end
    -- refresh window panel sheen
    if Window then
        local pg = Window:FindFirstChild("ThemePanelGradient")
        if pg and pg:IsA("UIGradient") and p.panelGradient then
            pg.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, p.panelGradient[1]),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, p.panelGradient[2]),
            }
        end
        local sheen = Window:FindFirstChild("ThemeTopSheen")
        if sheen then
            sheen.BackgroundColor3 = p.particle or Theme.Accent
        end
    end
    -- particle / orb recolor
    if UI.SpookDots then
        for _, dot in ipairs(UI.SpookDots) do
            if dot.frame then
                dot.frame.BackgroundColor3 = p.particle or Theme.Accent
            end
        end
    end
    if UI.OrbFrames then
        for i, o in ipairs(UI.OrbFrames) do
            if o then
                o.BackgroundColor3 = p.particle or Theme.Accent
            end
        end
    end
    if UI.Shimmer then
        UI.Shimmer.BackgroundColor3 = p.particle or Theme.Accent
    end
    if UI.VLight then
        UI.VLight.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(p.accent[1], p.accent[2], p.accent[3])
    end

    -- moon / orb
    if UI.Moon then
        UI.Moon.BackgroundColor3 = p.moon
        if mode == "cyberpunk" then
            UI.Moon.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
            if UI.MoonCut then UI.MoonCut.Visible = false end
        elseif mode == "galaxy" then
            UI.Moon.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
            if UI.MoonCut then UI.MoonCut.Visible = false end
        elseif mode == "christmas" then
            UI.Moon.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            if UI.MoonCut then UI.MoonCut.Visible = false end -- full snowball/moon
        else
            UI.Moon.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            if UI.MoonCut then
                UI.MoonCut.Visible = true
                UI.MoonCut.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background
            end
        end
    end
    if UI.MoonGlow then
        UI.MoonGlow.BackgroundColor3 = p.moonGlow
    end

    -- web / corner lines
    if UI.WebRoot then
        UI.WebRoot.Visible = (mode == "halloween" or mode == "vampire")
        local lineCol = (mode == "vampire") and Color3.fromRGB(120, 30, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(220, 200, 220)
        for _, line in ipairs(UI.WebLines or {}) do
            if line then line.BackgroundColor3 = lineCol end
        end
    end

    -- primary floaters
    if UI.Ghost then
        if mode == "halloween" then
            UI.Ghost.Text = "👻"
            UI.Ghost.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "christmas" then
            UI.Ghost.Text = "🎅"
            UI.Ghost.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "cyberpunk" then
            UI.Ghost.Text = "⚡"
            UI.Ghost.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "vampire" then
            UI.Ghost.Text = "🦇"
            UI.Ghost.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "galaxy" then
            UI.Ghost.Text = "✨"
            UI.Ghost.Visible = true
        end
    end
    if UI.Ghost2 then
        if mode == "halloween" then
            UI.Ghost2.Text = "👻"
            UI.Ghost2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "christmas" then
            UI.Ghost2.Text = "🎄"
            UI.Ghost2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "cyberpunk" then
            UI.Ghost2.Text = "◆"
            UI.Ghost2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "vampire" then
            UI.Ghost2.Text = "🩸"
            UI.Ghost2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "galaxy" then
            UI.Ghost2.Text = "🪐"
            UI.Ghost2.Visible = true
        end
    end

    -- bottom strip
    if UI.Pumpkins then
        local strip = {
            halloween = {"🎃", "🎃", "🎃", "🎃", "🎃"},
            christmas = {"🎅", "🎄", "🎅", "🎁", "🎅"},
            cyberpunk = {"⚡", "◆", "◇", "⚡", "◆"},
            vampire   = {"🦇", "🩸", "💀", "🦇", "🩸"},
            galaxy    = {"✨", "⭐", "💫", "✨", "🪐"},
        }
        local em = strip[mode] or strip.halloween
        for i, lab in ipairs(UI.Pumpkins) do
            if lab then lab.Text = em[((i - 1) % #em) + 1] end
        end
    end

    -- bats / secondary
    if UI.Bat1 then
        if mode == "halloween" or mode == "vampire" then
            UI.Bat1.Text = "🦇"; UI.Bat1.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "christmas" then
            UI.Bat1.Text = "❄️"; UI.Bat1.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "cyberpunk" then
            UI.Bat1.Text = "◇"; UI.Bat1.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "galaxy" then
            UI.Bat1.Text = "⭐"; UI.Bat1.Visible = true
        end
    end
    if UI.Bat2 then
        if mode == "halloween" or mode == "vampire" then
            UI.Bat2.Text = "🦇"; UI.Bat2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "christmas" then
            UI.Bat2.Text = "❄️"; UI.Bat2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "cyberpunk" then
            UI.Bat2.Text = "◆"; UI.Bat2.Visible = true
        elseif mode == "galaxy" then
            UI.Bat2.Text = "💫"; UI.Bat2.Visible = true
        end
    end

    -- extra floaters — Santa hats cascade for Christmas; themed for others
    if UI.ExtraFloats then
        local floats = p.floatEmojis or {"🎃"}
        local showExtra = (mode == "christmas" or mode == "galaxy" or mode == "cyberpunk")
        for i, row in ipairs(UI.ExtraFloats) do
            if row and row.frame then
                row.frame.Visible = showExtra
                row.frame.Text = floats[((i - 1) % #floats) + 1]
            end
        end
    end

    -- particle colors
    if UI.SpookDots then
        for _, dot in ipairs(UI.SpookDots) do
            if dot and dot.frame then
                dot.frame.BackgroundColor3 = p.particle
            end
        end
    end

    -- header / launcher emoji if present
    if UI.HeaderLogo and UI.HeaderLogo:IsA("TextLabel") then
        UI.HeaderLogo.Text = p.primaryEmoji or "🎃"
    end
    if UI.LauncherLogo and UI.LauncherLogo:IsA("TextLabel") then
        -- keep letter/icon if it's not emoji-based; only swap when emoji-sized
        if #(UI.LauncherLogo.Text or "") <= 4 then
            pcall(function() UI.LauncherLogo.Text = p.primaryEmoji end)
        end
    end
end

-- pulsing outline on window
UI.PulseStroke = Window:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
task.spawn(function()
    local t = 0
    while Master and Master.Parent do
        t = t + 0.02
        if UI.BgGradient then
            UI.BgGradient.Rotation = (120 + t * 18) % 360
        end
        if UI.Shimmer and UI.Shimmer.Parent then
            local sx = -140 + ((t * 55) % (WIN_W + 280))
            local sy = 20 + math.sin(t * 0.7) * 30
            UI.Shimmer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sx, sy)
        end
        if UI.VLight and UI.VLight.Parent then
            local vx = -90 + ((t * 35) % (WIN_W + 180))
            UI.VLight.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(vx, -40)
        end
        for i, o in ipairs(UI.OrbFrames or {}) do
            if o and o.Parent then
                local phase = t * (0.4 + i * 0.11) + i * 1.3
                o.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
                    20 + (i - 1) * 85 + math.sin(phase) * 55,
                    80 + i * 85 + math.cos(phase * 0.85) * 45
                )
                o.BackgroundTransparency = 0.84 + 0.08 * math.sin(phase * 1.2)
            end
        end
        -- floating primary
        if UI.Ghost and UI.Ghost.Parent and UI.Ghost.Visible then
            local gx = 18 + math.sin(t * 0.55) * 28
            local gy = 120 + math.sin(t * 0.9) * 18
            UI.Ghost.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(gx, gy)
            UI.Ghost.Rotation = -8 + math.sin(t * 0.7) * 10
            UI.Ghost.TextTransparency = 0.12 + 0.12 * math.sin(t * 1.1)
        end
        if UI.Ghost2 and UI.Ghost2.Parent and UI.Ghost2.Visible then
            local gx2 = 280 + math.cos(t * 0.4) * 22
            local gy2 = 200 + math.sin(t * 0.65) * 24
            UI.Ghost2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(gx2, gy2)
            UI.Ghost2.Rotation = 12 + math.cos(t * 0.5) * 14
            UI.Ghost2.TextTransparency = 0.28 + 0.15 * math.sin(t * 0.9)
        end
        -- secondary drift
        if UI.Bat1 and UI.Bat1.Parent and UI.Bat1.Visible then
            UI.Bat1.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(60 + math.sin(t * 0.8) * 40, 50 + math.cos(t * 1.1) * 12)
            UI.Bat1.Rotation = math.sin(t * 2) * 18
        end
        if UI.Bat2 and UI.Bat2.Parent and UI.Bat2.Visible then
            UI.Bat2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(240 + math.cos(t * 0.6) * 35, 90 + math.sin(t * 0.95) * 16)
            UI.Bat2.Rotation = math.cos(t * 1.7) * 20
        end
        -- extra floaters (Santa hats etc.)
        if UI.ExtraFloats then
            for i, row in ipairs(UI.ExtraFloats) do
                local f = row and row.frame
                if f and f.Parent and f.Visible then
                    local sp = row.speed or 0.5
                    local xx = row.ox + math.sin(t * sp + row.phase) * 30
                    local yy = row.oy + math.cos(t * sp * 0.9 + row.phase) * 22
                    f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(xx, yy)
                    f.Rotation = math.sin(t * 1.4 + row.phase) * 16
                    f.TextTransparency = 0.15 + 0.2 * math.abs(math.sin(t * sp))
                end
            end
        end
        -- moon pulse
        if UI.MoonGlow and UI.MoonGlow.Parent then
            UI.MoonGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78 + 0.08 * math.sin(t * 0.8)
        end
        -- bottom strip bob
        if UI.Pumpkins then
            for i, p in ipairs(UI.Pumpkins) do
                if p and p.Parent then
                    local bob = math.sin(t * 1.2 + i * 0.9) * 3
                    p.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12 + (i - 1) * 70, 4 + bob)
                    p.Rotation = math.sin(t * 0.8 + i) * 6
                end
            end
        end
        -- particles float up
        if UI.SpookDots then
            for _, dot in ipairs(UI.SpookDots) do
                local f = dot.frame
                if f and f.Parent then
                    local yy = (dot.oy - (t * 18 * dot.speed)) % 560
                    local xx = dot.ox + math.sin(t * dot.speed + dot.ox) * 12
                    f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(xx, yy)
                    f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 + 0.35 * math.abs(math.sin(t * dot.speed))
                end
            end
        end
        if UI.PulseStroke then
            UI.PulseStroke.Transparency = 0.35 + 0.25 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 1.5))
        end
        task.wait(0.03)
    end
end)

end)
-- hard-disable input on every decor/anim descendant so avatar grid always receives clicks
pcall(function()
    for _, layer in ipairs({ UI.DecoLayer, UI.AnimLayer, UI.BackgroundImage, UI.BackgroundShade }) do
        if layer then
            layer.Active = false
            pcall(function() layer.Interactable = false end)
            for _, d in ipairs(layer:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    d.Active = false
                    pcall(function() d.Interactable = false end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(b, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Row }, 0.12) end)
    return b
end

-- ACE-style pill toggle
local function makeAceToggle(parent, y, titleText, initial, onChange)
    local row = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, parent)
    local title = label(row, titleText, 12, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0)

    local track = new("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 26),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0.5, -13),
        BackgroundColor3 = initial and Theme.Accent or Theme.ToggleOff,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Active = true,
        Text = "",
    }, row)
    corner(track, 99)
    local knob = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
        Position = initial and UDim2.new(1, -23, 0.5, -10) or UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 240),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, track)
    corner(knob, 99)

    local state = initial == true
    local function render(v)
        state = v
        twSoft(track, { BackgroundColor3 = v and Theme.Accent or Theme.ToggleOff }, 0.22)
        twBounce(knob, {
            Position = v and UDim2.new(1, -23, 0.5, -10) or UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
        }, 0.28)
        -- micro pulse
        knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
        twBounce(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20) }, 0.2)
    end
    bindClick(track, function()
        render(not state)
        onChange(state)
    end)
    render(state)
    return render
end

-- SUBMIT AFTER row (1 / 2 / 3 / 4 words)

    local row = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 168),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, UI.MainSettings)
    local saTitle = label(row, "SUBMIT AFTER", 12, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
    saTitle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 36)
    saTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0)
    local hold = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 28),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -168, 0.5, -14),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, row)
    corner(hold, 8)
    local opts = {1, 2, 3, 4}
    for i, n in ipairs(opts) do
        local b = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 24),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4 + (i-1)*39, 2),
            BackgroundColor3 = (cfg.submitAfter == n) and Theme.Accent or Theme.ToggleOff,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Active = true,
            Text = tostring(n),
            TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        }, hold)
        corner(b, 6)
        bindClick(b, function()
            cfg.submitAfter = n
            clearCapture()
            saveConfig()
            for _, c in ipairs(hold:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("TextButton") then
                    c.BackgroundColor3 = (tonumber(c.Text) == n) and Theme.Accent or Theme.ToggleOff
                end
            end
            logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">submit after </font><font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. n .. " word(s)</font>")
        end)
    end
end

-- hide legacy panels that are replaced by ACE layout
local function hideLegacy(obj)
    if obj then obj.Visible = false; obj.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) end
end

    }, Body)

    stroke(holder, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.28)

    local modeTitle = label(holder, "REDEEM MODE", 11, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamBold)

    local modeNote = label(holder, "fragments required before submit", 8, Theme.Dim, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
    modeNote.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 18)
    modeNote.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 34)

    local selector = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(142, 44),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -156, 0.5, -22),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6, 6, 10),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, holder)
    corner(selector, 15)
    stroke(selector, Color3.fromRGB(60, 55, 80), 1, 0.15)

    local function modeButton(text, x)
        local button = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 38),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 3),
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Row,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Active = true,
            Text = text,
            TextSize = 17,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
        }, selector)
        corner(button, 12)
        return button
    end

    local minusButton = modeButton("-", 3)
    local valueLabel = label(selector, tostring(cfg.submitAfter), 17, Theme.Text,
        Enum.Font.GothamBlack, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
    valueLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 44)
    valueLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(45, 0)
    local plusButton = modeButton("+", 101)

    local function setMode(delta)
        local nextValue = math.clamp(cfg.submitAfter + delta, MIN, MAX)
        if nextValue == cfg.submitAfter then return end
        cfg.submitAfter = nextValue
        valueLabel.Text = tostring(nextValue)
        clearCapture()
        saveConfig()
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">mode: </font><font color="'
            .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. nextValue .. "/5</font>")
    end
    bindClick(minusButton, function() setMode(-1) end)
    bindClick(plusButton, function() setMode(1) end)
end

local function makeAdj(parent, x, title, getText, onDelta)
    local hold = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 52),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 54),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 6,
    }, parent)
    corner(hold, 12)
    stroke(hold, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.2)
    local t = label(hold, title, 8, Theme.Dim, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
    t.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
    t.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3)
    t.ZIndex = 7
    local val = new("TextLabel", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 24),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(35, 20),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = getText(),
        TextSize = 12,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        ZIndex = 7,
    }, hold)
    local function mkBtn(tx, px, delta)
        local b = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 26),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px, 19),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 48, 72),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            AutoButtonColor = true,
            Active = true,
            Text = tx,
            TextSize = 16,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
            ZIndex = 8,
        }, hold)
        corner(b, 8)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            onDelta(delta)
            val.Text = getText()
            saveConfig()
        end)
        b.Activated:Connect(function()
            onDelta(delta)
            val.Text = getText()
            saveConfig()
        end)
        return b
    end
    mkBtn("-", 4, -1)
    mkBtn("+", 84, 1)
    return val
end

toolBtn("OPEN CODES", 0, 90, function()
    pcall(function()
        local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end
        local codes = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
        if codes and codes:IsA("ScreenGui") then codes.Enabled = true end
        for _, btn in ipairs(findCodeButtons(pg)) do
            clickButton(btn)
        end
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">codes panel opened</font>')
    end)
end)

toolBtn("COPY CODE", 96, 84, function()
    local code = ""
    if UI.CodeBox and UI.CodeBox.Text ~= "" then code = UI.CodeBox.Text end
    if code == "" and _lastNonBlankText then code = _lastNonBlankText end
    if code == "" then
        setStatus("nothing to copy", LOG.warn)
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        if setclipboard then setclipboard(code)
        elseif toclipboard then toclipboard(code)
        elseif Clipboard and Clipboard.set then Clipboard.set(code)
        end
    end)
    setStatus("COPIED  •  " .. code, LOG.ok)
end)

toolBtn("CLEAR CAP", 186, 84, function()
    clearCapture()
    if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = "" end
    setStatus("capture cleared", LOG.dim)
end)

toolBtn("FORCE SUB", 276, 90, function()
    local code = trim(UI.CodeBox and UI.CodeBox.Text or "")
    if code == "" then
        setStatus("type a code first", LOG.warn)
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, msg = typeAndSubmitCode(code)
        setStatus((ok and "FORCE OK  •  " or "FORCE FAIL  •  ") .. code, ok and LOG.ok or LOG.err)
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">force: </font><font color="'
            .. (ok and LOG.ok or LOG.err) .. '">' .. tostring(msg) .. "</font>")
    end)
end)

UI.DelayMinus, UI.DelayPlus, UI.DelayBox = makeValueControl(48, "REDEEM DELAY", "0 = instant (as fast as possible)", string.format("%.4f", cfg.redeemDelay))
local function setRedeemDelay(value)
    cfg.redeemDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or cfg.redeemDelay, 0, 3.00)
    -- show up to 4 decimals so tiny values are visible
    if cfg.redeemDelay == 0 then
        UI.DelayBox.Text = "0"
    elseif cfg.redeemDelay < 0.01 then
        UI.DelayBox.Text = string.format("%.4f", cfg.redeemDelay)
    else
        UI.DelayBox.Text = string.format("%.3f", cfg.redeemDelay)
    end
    saveConfig()
end
bindClick(UI.DelayMinus, function()
    local step = 0.01
    if cfg.redeemDelay <= 0.01 then step = 0.001 end
    if cfg.redeemDelay <= 0.001 then step = 0.0001 end
    setRedeemDelay(math.max(0, cfg.redeemDelay - step))
end)
bindClick(UI.DelayPlus, function()
    local step = 0.0001
    if cfg.redeemDelay >= 0.001 then step = 0.001 end
    if cfg.redeemDelay >= 0.01 then step = 0.01 end
    setRedeemDelay(cfg.redeemDelay + step)
end)
UI.DelayBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
    setRedeemDelay(tonumber(UI.DelayBox.Text) or cfg.redeemDelay)
end)
UI.DelayBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() setRedeemDelay(UI.DelayBox.Text) end)

UI.SizeMinus, UI.SizePlus, UI.SizeBox = makeValueControl(120, "MENU SIZE", "scale the full redeemer", tostring(math.floor(cfg.menuScale * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%")
local function setMenuScale(value)
    local numeric = tonumber(tostring(value):gsub("%%", ""))
    if numeric and numeric > 2 then numeric = numeric / 100 end
    cfg.menuScale = math.clamp(numeric or cfg.menuScale, 0.70, 1.25)
    cfg.menuScale = math.floor(cfg.menuScale * 20 + 0.5) / 20
    UI.SizeBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(cfg.menuScale * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"
    tw(MenuScale, { Scale = cfg.menuScale }, 0.16)
    saveConfig()
end
bindClick(UI.SizeMinus, function() setMenuScale(cfg.menuScale - 0.05) end)
bindClick(UI.SizePlus, function() setMenuScale(cfg.menuScale + 0.05) end)
UI.SizeBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() setMenuScale(UI.SizeBox.Text) end)

    local row = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 62),

    local title = label(row, titleText, 12, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamBlack)
    title.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 24)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 9)
    local note = label(row, noteText, 8, Theme.Dim, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
    note.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, 18)
    note.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 32)
    local button = new("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(88, 40),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -102, 0.5, -20),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 31, 37),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Active = true,
        Text = "OFF",
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        TextSize = 11,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack,
    }, row)
    corner(button, 13)
    local state = false
    bindClick(button, function()
        state = not state
        tw(button, { BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(246, 246, 248) or Color3.fromRGB(31, 31, 37) }, 0.13)
        tw(rowStroke, { Color = state and HEADLESS_PURPLE or Color3.fromRGB(80, 40, 20) }, 0.13)
        button.Text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
        button.TextColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 19) or Theme.Text
        onChange(state)
    end)
    return button
end

-- XRAY BASES (lightweight LocalTransparencyModifier — no lag)
local xrayOn = false
local xrayParts = {} -- [part] = previous LocalTransparencyModifier
local function setHelperXray(state)
    xrayOn = state == true
    if not xrayOn then
        for part, prev in pairs(xrayParts) do
            pcall(function()
                if part and part.Parent then
                    part.LocalTransparencyModifier = prev or 0
                end
            end)
        end
        for k in pairs(xrayParts) do xrayParts[k] = nil end
        return
    end
    -- one-shot scan (not every frame) — skip character parts
    local char = player.Character
    local n = 0
    for _, inst in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if inst:IsA("BasePart") and inst.Transparency < 0.95 then
            if not (char and inst:IsDescendantOf(char)) then
                if xrayParts[inst] == nil then
                    xrayParts[inst] = inst.LocalTransparencyModifier
                end
                inst.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.65
                n = n + 1
                if n % 200 == 0 then task.wait() end -- yield so no freeze
            end
        end
    end
end

makeHelperToggle(548, "ANTI SIT", "force stand if seated", setHelperAntiSit)
makeHelperToggle(608, "XRAY BASES", "see through map / bases", setHelperXray)

do

UI.HelperHint = label(UI.HelperPage, "FARM HELPERS RUN ONLY WHILE ENABLED  •  SAFE MODE PREVENTS FAST-SUBMIT KICKS", 8, Theme.Dim,
    Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)

local function showPage(name)
    Body.Visible = name == "MAIN"
    UI.HelperPage.Visible = name == "FARM"
    if UI.AvatarPage then
        UI.AvatarPage.Visible = name == "AVATAR"
        if name == "AVATAR" then
            -- ensure overlays cannot intercept clicks on avatar grid
            pcall(function()
                if UI.DecoLayer then UI.DecoLayer.Active = false; UI.DecoLayer.ZIndex = 2 end
                if UI.AnimLayer then UI.AnimLayer.Active = false; UI.AnimLayer.ZIndex = 2 end
                UI.AvatarPage.ZIndex = 20
                for _, d in ipairs(UI.AvatarPage:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("ImageButton") or d:IsA("TextButton") then
                        d.Active = true
                        d.ZIndex = math.max(d.ZIndex, 25)
                        pcall(function() d.Interactable = true end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    if UI.LogsPage then UI.LogsPage.Visible = false end
    if UI.SettingsPage then UI.SettingsPage.Visible = name == "SETTINGS" end
    local active = Theme.Accent
    local idle = Theme.Panel
    local order = { "MAIN", "FARM", "AVATAR", "SETTINGS" }
    local tabs = {
        MAIN = UI.MainTab,
        FARM = UI.FarmTab,
        AVATAR = UI.AvatarTab,
        SETTINGS = UI.SettingsTab,
    }
    local idx = 1
    for i, key in ipairs(order) do
        if key == name then idx = i end
        local tab = tabs[key]
        if tab then
            local on = key == name
            tw(tab, { BackgroundColor3 = on and active or idle }, 0.14)
            tab.TextColor3 = on and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
        end
    end
    if UI.TabIndicator then
        local x = (idx - 1) * 0.25
        tw(UI.TabIndicator, { Position = UDim2.new(x, 6, 0, 34), BackgroundColor3 = HEADLESS_PURPLE }, 0.18)
    end
end
bindClick(UI.MainTab, function() showPage("MAIN") end)
bindClick(UI.FarmTab, function() showPage("FARM") end)
if UI.AvatarTab then bindClick(UI.AvatarTab, function() showPage("AVATAR") end) end
bindClick(UI.SettingsTab, function() showPage("SETTINGS") end)
showPage("MAIN")

-- equipped state: [assetId] = accessory instance or true
local equippedItems = {}
local savedOriginalDesc = nil -- HumanoidDescription snapshot
local function saveOriginalAvatar()
    if savedOriginalDesc then return end
    local _, hum = getCharHum()
    if not hum then return end
    pcall(function()
        savedOriginalDesc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
    end)
end
task.defer(function()
    task.wait(1)
    saveOriginalAvatar()
end)
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(1)
    if not savedOriginalDesc then saveOriginalAvatar() end
end)
