local function getCharHum()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return char, hum
end

local function removeEquipped(assetId)
    local tag = "HHAvatar_" .. tostring(assetId)
    local char, hum = getCharHum()
    if char then
        for _, c in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if c.Name == tag or (c:GetAttribute("HHAvatarId") == assetId) then
                pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if c:GetAttribute("HHAvatarId") == assetId then
                pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        -- restore headless / face
        if assetId == 15093053680 or assetId == 201 then
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                head.Transparency = 0
                for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                    if d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then d.Transparency = 0 end
                end
            end
        end
        -- restore HumanoidDescription hat list if we stored previous
        if equippedItems[assetId] == "desc" and hum then
            pcall(function()
                local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
                local hats = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
                local out = {}
                for part in string.gmatch(hats, "[^,]+") do
                    if tostring(part) ~= tostring(assetId) then
                        out[#out + 1] = part
                    end
                end
                desc.HatAccessory = table.concat(out, ",")
                if hum.ApplyDescriptionClient then
                    hum:ApplyDescriptionClient(desc)
                else
                    hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    equippedItems[assetId] = nil
end

local function applyHeadless(char)
    local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end
    head.Transparency = 1
    for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
            d.Transparency = 1
        end
    end
end

local function parentAccessoryToCharacter(item, char, hum, tag, assetId)
    item.Name = tag
    pcall(function() item:SetAttribute("HHAvatarId", assetId) end)
    for _, d in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            d.Anchored = false
            d.CanCollide = false
            d.Massless = true
        end
    end
    local handle = item:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    -- Prefer real AddAccessory, then force weld using matching Attachment
    pcall(function() hum:AddAccessory(item) end)
    if not item.Parent then item.Parent = char end

    if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
        local hatAtt = handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
        local bodyPart, bodyAtt
        if hatAtt then
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local m = part:FindFirstChild(hatAtt.Name)
                    if m and m:IsA("Attachment") then
                        bodyPart, bodyAtt = part, m
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        bodyPart = bodyPart or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if bodyPart then
            for _, w in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
                if w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint") or w:IsA("ManualWeld") then
                    pcall(function() w:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
            local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
            weld.Name = "HHAvatarWeld"
            weld.Part0 = bodyPart
            weld.Part1 = handle
            if bodyAtt and hatAtt then
                weld.C0 = bodyAtt.CFrame
                weld.C1 = hatAtt.CFrame
            else
                weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.6, 0)
                weld.C1 = CFrame.new()
            end
            weld.Parent = handle
        end
    end
    return item.Parent ~= nil
end

local function tryLoadModel(assetId)
    local id = tonumber(assetId) or assetId
    local function firstObj(objs)
        if type(objs) == "table" and objs[1] then return objs[1] end
        if typeof(objs) == "Instance" then return objs end
        return nil
    end
    -- 1) executor GetObjects
    local ok, objs = pcall(function()
        if typeof(game.GetObjects) == "function" then
            return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id))
        end
        return nil
    end)
    local got = firstObj(ok and objs)
    if got then return got end
    -- 2) global getobjects / getobject
    for _, fnName in ipairs({ "getobjects", "getobject", "GetObjects" }) do
        local fn = rawget(env, fnName) or rawget(_G, fnName)
        if type(fn) == "function" then
            ok, objs = pcall(fn, "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id))
            got = firstObj(ok and objs)
            if got then return got end
        end
    end
    -- 3) InsertService LoadLocalAsset (client-friendly when available)
    ok, objs = pcall(function()
        local ins = game:GetService("InsertService")
        if ins and type(ins.LoadLocalAsset) == "function" then
            return ins:LoadLocalAsset("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id))
        end
        return nil
    end)
    got = firstObj(ok and objs)
    if got then return got end
    -- 4) InsertService LoadAsset
    ok, objs = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(id)
    end)
    got = firstObj(ok and objs)
    if got then return got end
    -- 5) LoadAssetVersion
    ok, objs = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAssetVersion(id)
    end)
    got = firstObj(ok and objs)
    if got then return got end
    return nil
end

local function equipFromModel(model, char, hum, tag, assetId)
    if not model then return false end
    local added = false
    local function wear(item)
        local clone = item:Clone()
        if parentAccessoryToCharacter(clone, char, hum, tag, assetId) then
            added = true
        end
    end
    if model:IsA("Accessory") or model:IsA("Hat") or model:IsA("Accoutrement") then
        wear(model)
    else
        for _, item in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Accessory") or item:IsA("Hat") or item:IsA("Accoutrement") then
                wear(item)
            end
        end
        if not added then
            for _, item in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if item:IsA("Accessory") or item:IsA("Hat") then
                    wear(item)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function() if model then model:Destroy() end end)
    return added
end

local function equipViaDescription(hum, assetId, field)
    -- field: "HatAccessory" | "Face" | "RightLeg" | "LeftLeg"
    field = field or "HatAccessory"
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
        if field == "HatAccessory" then
            local hats = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
            if hats == "" then
                desc.HatAccessory = tostring(assetId)
            else
                -- avoid dup
                if not string.find("," .. hats .. ",", "," .. tostring(assetId) .. ",", 1, true) then
                    desc.HatAccessory = hats .. "," .. tostring(assetId)
                end
            end
        elseif field == "Face" then
            desc.Face = tonumber(assetId) or 0
        elseif field == "RightLeg" then
            desc.RightLeg = tonumber(assetId) or 0
        elseif field == "LeftLeg" then
            desc.LeftLeg = tonumber(assetId) or 0
        end
        if hum.ApplyDescriptionClient then
            hum:ApplyDescriptionClient(desc)
        else
            hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
        end
    end)
    return ok
end

-- Hard-apply a HumanoidDescription on the LOCAL player only
local function applyDescLocal(hum, desc)
    if not hum or not desc then return false end
    local applied = false
    -- Method A: client apply APIs
    for _, fn in ipairs({ "ApplyDescriptionClient", "ApplyDescriptionAsync", "ApplyDescription" }) do
        if not applied and hum[fn] then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                if fn == "ApplyDescriptionAsync" then
                    hum:ApplyDescriptionAsync(desc)
                elseif fn == "ApplyDescriptionClient" then
                    hum:ApplyDescriptionClient(desc)
                else
                    hum:ApplyDescription(desc)
                end
            end)
            if ok then applied = true end
        end
    end
    -- Method B: rebuild character model from description (most reliable for body parts)
    if not applied or true then
        local okModel, newModel = pcall(function()
            local rig = hum.RigType or Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
            return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescriptionAsync(desc, rig)
        end)
        if okModel and newModel then
            local okSwap = pcall(function()
                local old = player.Character
                local pivot = old and old:GetPivot() or CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)
                newModel.Name = player.Name
                newModel.Parent = workspace
                player.Character = newModel
                pcall(function() newModel:PivotTo(pivot) end)
                local nh = newModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if nh then
                    nh.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
                end
                if old and old ~= newModel then
                    task.defer(function() pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end)
                end
            end)
            if okSwap then applied = true end
        end
    end
    return applied
end

-- Manually load every accessory id listed on a description (local only)
local function wearDescAccessories(char, hum, desc)
    if not char or not desc then return end
    local fields = {
        "HatAccessory", "HairAccessory", "FaceAccessory", "NeckAccessory",
        "ShouldersAccessory", "FrontAccessory", "BackAccessory", "WaistAccessory",
    }
    for _, field in ipairs(fields) do
        local raw = tostring(desc[field] or "")
        for idStr in string.gmatch(raw, "%d+") do
            local id = tonumber(idStr)
            if id then
                local model = tryLoadModel(id)
                if model then
                    local tag = "HHAvatar_" .. tostring(id)
                    equipFromModel(model, char, hum, tag, id)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function equipAsset(assetId, kind)
    local char, hum = getCharHum()
    if not char or not hum then return false end
    saveOriginalAvatar()
    local tag = "HHAvatar_" .. tostring(assetId)
    assetId = tonumber(assetId) or assetId

    if equippedItems[assetId] then
        removeEquipped(assetId)
        -- if body part was applied via full desc, reset
        if equippedItems[assetId] == nil and savedOriginalDesc then
            applyDescLocal(hum, savedOriginalDesc)
        end
        return false
    end

    -- Headless
    if assetId == 15093053680 or kind == "headless" or (kind == "bundle" and assetId == 201) then
        applyHeadless(char)
        equippedItems[assetId] = true
        return true
    end

    -- Face
    if assetId == 158380697314856 then
        local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
        desc.Face = assetId
        if applyDescLocal(hum, desc) then
            equippedItems[assetId] = "desc"
            return true
        end
    end

    -- Korblox legs / bundle — body parts need full description apply
    if assetId == 139607718 or assetId == 139607673 or (kind == "bundle" and assetId == 192) then
        local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
        if assetId == 139607718 or assetId == 192 then
            desc.RightLeg = 139607718
        end
        if assetId == 139607673 or assetId == 192 then
            desc.LeftLeg = 139607673
        end
        -- known Korblox Deathspeaker-related extras when full bundle
        if assetId == 192 then
            pcall(function()
                -- torso/arms if these properties exist on description
                if desc.Torso == 0 or true then
                    -- keep current torso; legs are the main visual
                end
            end)
        end
        if applyDescLocal(hum, desc) then
            equippedItems[assetId] = "desc"
            -- re-fetch character after possible swap
            char, hum = getCharHum()
            return true
        end
        -- fallback: try loading as accessory model anyway
        local model = tryLoadModel(assetId == 192 and 139607718 or assetId)
        if model and equipFromModel(model, char, hum, tag, assetId) then
            equippedItems[assetId] = true
            return true
        end
    end

    -- Hats / limiteds: try HumanoidDescription FIRST (works client-side without ownership)
    do
        local desc = hum:GetAppliedDescription()
        local hats = tostring(desc.HatAccessory or "")
        if hats == "" then
            desc.HatAccessory = tostring(assetId)
        elseif not string.find("," .. hats .. ",", "," .. tostring(assetId) .. ",", 1, true) then
            desc.HatAccessory = hats .. "," .. tostring(assetId)
        end
        if applyDescLocal(hum, desc) then
            equippedItems[assetId] = "desc"
            -- also try physical model for engines that strip desc hats
            task.spawn(function()
                local ch2, hum2 = getCharHum()
                if not ch2 then return end
                local m2 = tryLoadModel(assetId)
                if m2 then equipFromModel(m2, ch2, hum2, tag, assetId) end
            end)
            return true
        end
    end

    -- Fallback: load model + weld
    local model = tryLoadModel(assetId)
    if model and equipFromModel(model, char, hum, tag, assetId) then
        equippedItems[assetId] = true
        return true
    end

    -- Last resort: force-add via Accessory instance shell
    local okForce = pcall(function()
        local acc = Instance.new("Accessory")
        acc.Name = tag
        acc:SetAttribute("HHAvatarId", assetId)
        local handle = Instance.new("Part")
        handle.Name = "Handle"
        handle.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        handle.Transparency = 1
        handle.CanCollide = false
        handle.Massless = true
        handle.Parent = acc
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(assetId)
        mesh.Parent = handle
        local hatAtt = Instance.new("Attachment")
        hatAtt.Name = "HatAttachment"
        hatAtt.Parent = handle
        parentAccessoryToCharacter(acc, char, hum, tag, assetId)
    end)
    if okForce and char:FindFirstChild(tag) then
        equippedItems[assetId] = true
        return true
    end

    return false
end

-- Copy another user's full avatar onto LOCAL character only
local function copyAvatarByUsername(username)
    username = trim(tostring(username or "")):gsub("^@", "")
    if username == "" then return false, "empty username" end
    saveOriginalAvatar()
    local char, hum = getCharHum()
    if not hum then return false, "no character" end

    local okId, userId = pcall(function()
        return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(username)
    end)
    if not okId or not userId or userId == 0 then
        -- try numeric id
        userId = tonumber(username)
        if not userId then return false, "user not found" end
    end

    local okDesc, desc = pcall(function()
        return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(userId)
    end)
    if not okDesc or not desc then return false, "no description" end

    -- Primary: hard apply (includes character rebuild)
    local okApply = applyDescLocal(hum, desc)
    char, hum = getCharHum()

    -- Secondary: manually wear every accessory from the description
    if char and hum then
        wearDescAccessories(char, hum, desc)
    end

    if not okApply and not char then
        return false, "apply failed"
    end

    for k in pairs(equippedItems) do equippedItems[k] = nil end
    return true, "copied " .. username
end

local function resetOwnAvatar()
    local char, hum = getCharHum()
    if not hum then return false end
    if char then
        for _, c in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if type(c.Name) == "string" and c.Name:sub(1, 9) == "HHAvatar_" then
                pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            head.Transparency = 0
            for _, d in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                if d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then d.Transparency = 0 end
            end
        end
    end
    for k in pairs(equippedItems) do equippedItems[k] = nil end
    if savedOriginalDesc then
        applyDescLocal(hum, savedOriginalDesc)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

bindClick(copyBtn, function()
    local name = copyBox.Text
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, msg = copyAvatarByUsername(name)
        if ok then
            setStatus("AVATAR  •  copied " .. tostring(name), LOG.ok)
            logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">copied avatar: ' .. tostring(name) .. "</font>")
        else
            setStatus("AVATAR  •  " .. tostring(msg), LOG.err)
        end
    end)
end)
bindClick(resetAvBtn, function()
    task.spawn(function()
        if resetOwnAvatar() then
            setStatus("AVATAR  •  reset to original", LOG.ok)
        else
            setStatus("AVATAR  •  nothing saved yet", LOG.warn)
        end
    end)
end)
copyBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
    if enter then
        task.spawn(function() copyAvatarByUsername(copyBox.Text) end)
    end
end)

-- grid of image buttons
local grid = new("Frame", {
    Name = "AvatarGrid",
    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 86),
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
    ZIndex = 22,
    Active = true,
}, UI.AvatarPage)
local gridLayout = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
gridLayout.CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 72)
gridLayout.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
gridLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
gridLayout.Parent = grid

for i, item in ipairs(AVATAR_ITEMS) do
    local id = item.id
    local kind = item.kind or "asset"
    local btn = new("ImageButton", {
        Name = "AvatarItem_" .. tostring(id),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AutoButtonColor = true,
        Active = true,
        Selectable = true,
        ZIndex = 25,
        Image = (kind == "bundle")
            and ("rbxthumb://type=BundleThumbnail&id=" .. id .. "&w=150&h=150")
            or ("rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. id .. "&w=150&h=150"),
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        LayoutOrder = i,
    }, grid)
    pcall(function() btn.Interactable = true end)
    corner(btn, 12)
    local st = stroke(btn, Theme.Stroke, 1.5, 0.35)
    local glow = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 4),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -4),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Active = false,
        Selectable = false,
        ZIndex = 26,
    }, btn)
    pcall(function() glow.Interactable = false end)
    -- hover feedback
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not equippedItems[id] then
            tw(btn, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.RowHover or Theme.Row }, 0.12)
            st.Transparency = 0.15
        end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not equippedItems[id] then
            tw(btn, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel }, 0.12)
            st.Transparency = 0.35
        end
    end)

    local _equipBusy = false
    local function onEquipClick()
        if _equipBusy then return end
        _equipBusy = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, on = pcall(function()
                return equipAsset(id, kind)
            end)
            _equipBusy = false
            if not ok then
                setStatus("AVATAR  •  equip error", LOG.err)
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">equip error: ' .. tostring(on) .. "</font>")
                return
            end
            if on then
                st.Color = Theme.Accent
                st.Transparency = 0.05
                glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                    math.min(255, math.floor((cfg.accentR or 255) / 4) + 20),
                    math.min(255, math.floor((cfg.accentG or 120) / 4) + 12),
                    math.min(255, math.floor((cfg.accentB or 30) / 4) + 8)
                )
                setStatus("AVATAR  •  equipped #" .. tostring(id), LOG.ok)
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">equipped asset ' .. tostring(id) .. "</font>")
            else
                st.Color = Theme.Stroke
                st.Transparency = 0.35
                glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
                setStatus("AVATAR  •  removed #" .. tostring(id), LOG.dim)
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">removed asset ' .. tostring(id) .. "</font>")
            end
        end)
    end
    -- Single reliable click path (bindClick already covers mouse + touch)
    bindClick(btn, onEquipClick)
end

-- re-apply headless on respawn if was equipped
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    if equippedItems[15093053680] or equippedItems[201] then
        applyHeadless(char)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local t = 0
    while Master and Master.Parent do
        t = t + 0.025
        if UI.SettingsPage and UI.SettingsPage.Visible then
            if UI.SettingsShimmer then
                local x = -100 + ((t * 50) % 520)
                UI.SettingsShimmer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 10 + math.sin(t) * 12)
            end
            if UI.SettingsBar then
                local w = 40 + (math.sin(t * 1.2) * 0.5 + 0.5) * 160
                UI.SettingsBar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 2)
            end
            if UI.SettingsGlow then
                UI.SettingsGlow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
                    200 + math.sin(t * 0.6) * 80,
                    280 + math.cos(t * 0.5) * 60
                )
                UI.SettingsGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88 + 0.06 * math.sin(t * 1.4)
            end
            -- pulse color swatches if present
            for _, name in ipairs({ "bgSwatch", "panelSwatch", "accentSwatch" }) do
                -- swatches are local; pulse SettingsPage panel strokes instead
            end
            for _, d in ipairs(UI.SettingsPage:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("UIStroke") and d.Parent and d.Parent:IsA("Frame") then
                    if d.Parent.Size.Y.Offset >= 50 then
                        d.Transparency = 0.35 + 0.2 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 1.3 + (d.Parent.AbsolutePosition.Y or 0) * 0.01))
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.04)
    end
end)

UI.PresetButtons = {}
local function refreshPresetButtons()
    local cur = tostring(cfg.themePreset or "halloween")
    for name, btn in pairs(UI.PresetButtons) do
        if btn then
            local on = (name == cur)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Accent or Theme.Row
            btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Theme.Text
            local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if st then
                st.Color = on and Theme.Accent or Theme.Stroke
                st.Transparency = on and 0.1 or 0.4
            end
        end
    end
end

local function selectThemePreset(name)
    if not THEME_PRESETS[name] then return end
    applyPresetColors(name)
    -- sync color boxes if they exist
    pcall(function()
        if bgBoxes and bgKeys then
            for i, k in ipairs(bgKeys) do bgBoxes[i].Text = tostring(cfg[k]) end
        end
        if panelBoxes and panelKeys then
            for i, k in ipairs(panelKeys) do panelBoxes[i].Text = tostring(cfg[k]) end
        end
        if accentBoxes and accentKeys then
            for i, k in ipairs(accentKeys) do accentBoxes[i].Text = tostring(cfg[k]) end
        end
    end)
    saveConfig()
    if type(applyTheme) == "function" then
        applyTheme()
    end
    if type(applyDecorTheme) == "function" then
        applyDecorTheme(name)
    end
    refreshPresetButtons()
    if type(logRich) == "function" then
        local p = THEME_PRESETS[name]
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">theme: ' .. (p and p.label or name) .. " " .. (p and p.emoji or "") .. "</font>")
    end
    if type(setStatus) == "function" then
        setStatus("THEME  •  " .. string.upper(name), LOG.ok)
    end
end

for i, name in ipairs(PRESET_ORDER) do
    local p = THEME_PRESETS[name]
    local x = 10 + ((i - 1) % 5) * 68
    local y = 46
    local btn = new("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 36),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Row,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Active = true,
        Text = (p and p.emoji or "?") .. "\n" .. string.upper(string.sub(name, 1, 5)),
        TextSize = 9,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextWrapped = true,
    }, UI.PresetRow)
    corner(btn, 10)
    stroke(btn, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.4)
    UI.PresetButtons[name] = btn
    bindClick(btn, function()
        selectThemePreset(name)
    end)
end
task.defer(refreshPresetButtons)

local function makeColorRow(y, titleText, noteText, rKey, gKey, bKey)
    local row = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 78),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, UI.SettingsPage)
    corner(row, 16)
    stroke(row, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.4)
    local title = label(row, titleText, 11, Theme.Text, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
    title.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 20)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 8)
    local note = label(row, noteText, 8, Theme.Dim, Enum.Font.GothamMedium)
    note.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 16)
    note.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 28)

    local swatch = new("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 10),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(cfg[rKey], cfg[gKey], cfg[bKey]),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, row)
    corner(swatch, 8)
    stroke(swatch, Theme.Accent, 1, 0.3)

    local boxes = {}
    local labels_ch = { "R", "G", "B" }
    local keys = { rKey, gKey, bKey }
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local x = 14 + (i - 1) * 108
        local ch = label(row, labels_ch[i], 9, Theme.Dim, Enum.Font.GothamBold)
        ch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 22)
        ch.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 48)
        local box = new("TextBox", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 24),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + 16, 46),
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            Text = tostring(cfg[keys[i]]),
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextSize = 11,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
        }, row)
        corner(box, 8)
        stroke(box, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.35)
        boxes[i] = box
    end
    return row, swatch, boxes, keys
end

local bgRow, bgSwatch, bgBoxes, bgKeys = makeColorRow(150, "UI BACKGROUND", "main window / launcher base", "bgR", "bgG", "bgB")
local panelRow, panelSwatch, panelBoxes, panelKeys = makeColorRow(238, "PANEL / CARDS", "cards, rows, tab idle", "panelR", "panelG", "panelB")
local accentRow, accentSwatch, accentBoxes, accentKeys = makeColorRow(326, "ACCENT", "toggles, strokes, H icon", "accentR", "accentG", "accentB")

local themeTargets = {} -- filled by applyTheme registration

local function rebuildThemeFromCfg()
    HEADLESS_PURPLE = Color3.fromRGB(cfg.accentR, cfg.accentG, cfg.accentB)
    Theme.MainBackground = Color3.fromRGB(cfg.bgR, cfg.bgG, cfg.bgB)
    Theme.Background = Theme.MainBackground
    Theme.Panel = Color3.fromRGB(cfg.panelR, cfg.panelG, cfg.panelB)
    Theme.Row = Color3.fromRGB(math.min(255, cfg.panelR + 18), math.min(255, cfg.panelG + 12), math.min(255, cfg.panelB + 22))
    Theme.RowHover = Color3.fromRGB(math.min(255, cfg.panelR + 30), math.min(255, cfg.panelG + 22), math.min(255, cfg.panelB + 36))
    Theme.Accent = HEADLESS_PURPLE
    Theme.AccentLight = Color3.fromRGB(math.max(0, cfg.accentR - 40), math.max(0, cfg.accentG - 25), math.max(0, cfg.accentB - 40))
    Theme.SoftAccent = Color3.fromRGB(math.max(0, cfg.accentR - 90), math.max(0, cfg.accentG - 50), math.max(0, cfg.accentB - 90))
    Theme.Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(math.min(255, cfg.panelR + 30), math.min(255, cfg.panelG + 20), math.min(255, cfg.panelB + 40))
    Theme.InputBg = Color3.fromRGB(math.max(0, cfg.bgR - 8), math.max(0, cfg.bgG - 4), math.max(0, cfg.bgB - 6))
    Theme.SoftButton = Theme.Panel
    Theme.SoftButtonHover = Theme.RowHover
    LOG.acc = string.format("rgb(%d,%d,%d)", cfg.accentR, cfg.accentG, cfg.accentB)
end

local function applyTheme()
    rebuildThemeFromCfg()
    pcall(function()
        Window.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background
        if UI.BackgroundImage then UI.BackgroundImage.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background end
        if UI.BackgroundShade then UI.BackgroundShade.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.MainBackground end
        if UI.WindowOutline then UI.WindowOutline.Color = Theme.AccentLight end
        if UI.TabBar then UI.TabBar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg end
        if UI.LauncherLogo then UI.LauncherLogo.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent end
        if UI.HeaderLogo then UI.HeaderLogo.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent end
        if UI.LauncherLogoFrame then UI.LauncherLogoFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftAccent end
        if UI.HeaderLogoFrame then UI.HeaderLogoFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftAccent end
        if Launcher then Launcher.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Background end
        if UI.LauncherInner then UI.LauncherInner.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.MainBackground end
        if UI.LauncherGlow then UI.LauncherGlow.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent end
        if UI.LauncherShadow then UI.LauncherShadow.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent end
        if UI.HeaderDivider then UI.HeaderDivider.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.AccentLight end
        if UI.Console then UI.Console.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg end
        if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg end
        if UI.AskBox then UI.AskBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg end
        if UI.Footer then UI.Footer.TextColor3 = Theme.Dim end
        if MenuBtn then
            local s = MenuBtn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if s then s.Color = Theme.Accent end
        end
        if bgSwatch then bgSwatch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(cfg.bgR, cfg.bgG, cfg.bgB) end
        if panelSwatch then panelSwatch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(cfg.panelR, cfg.panelG, cfg.panelB) end
        if accentSwatch then accentSwatch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(cfg.accentR, cfg.accentG, cfg.accentB) end
        -- recolor solid panels / cards under the menu
        for _, d in ipairs(Window:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and d.BackgroundTransparency < 0.5 then
                local n = d.Name
                if n ~= "Power" and n ~= "BackgroundImage" and n ~= "BackgroundShade"
                    and n ~= "Header" and n ~= "MainPage" and n ~= "HelperPage" and n ~= "SettingsPage"
                    and n ~= "Tabs" and n ~= "LiveDot" and n ~= "PowerDot" and n ~= "RedDecoration" then
                    if d.Size.Y.Offset >= 50 or d.Size.Y.Scale > 0 then
                        d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
                    end
                end
            end
            if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                local parent = d.Parent
                if parent and parent ~= Power and parent.Name ~= "Power" then
                    if d.Thickness >= 1 then
                        d.Color = Theme.Stroke
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if type(applyPowerVisual) == "function" then applyPowerVisual() end
        if type(applyDecorTheme) == "function" then
            pcall(applyDecorTheme, cfg.themePreset or "halloween")
        end
        if type(refreshPresetButtons) == "function" then
            pcall(refreshPresetButtons)
        end
        local page = "MAIN"
        if UI.HelperPage and UI.HelperPage.Visible then page = "FARM" end
        if UI.AvatarPage and UI.AvatarPage.Visible then page = "AVATAR" end
        if UI.SettingsPage and UI.SettingsPage.Visible then page = "SETTINGS" end
        showPage(page)
    end)
end

local function commitColorBoxes(boxes, keys)
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local n = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(boxes[i].Text) or cfg[keys[i]]), 0, 255)
        cfg[keys[i]] = n
        boxes[i].Text = tostring(n)
    end
    saveConfig()
    applyTheme()
end

for _, pack in ipairs({
    { bgBoxes, bgKeys },
    { panelBoxes, panelKeys },
    { accentBoxes, accentKeys },
}) do
    for _, box in ipairs(pack[1]) do
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            commitColorBoxes(pack[1], pack[2])
        end)
    end
end
