-- console writers
local logLines = {}
local MAX_LOG = 160

local function scrollToBottom()
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait()
        if not UI.Console or not ConsoleText then return end
        local height = ConsoleText.AbsoluteSize.Y + 12
        UI.Console.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, height)
        UI.Console.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, height - UI.Console.AbsoluteSize.Y))
    end)
end
ConsoleText:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
    UI.Console.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ConsoleText.AbsoluteSize.Y + 12)
end)

logRich = function(line)
    logLines[#logLines + 1] = line
    if #logLines > MAX_LOG then table.remove(logLines, 1) end
    local joined = table.concat(logLines, "\n")
    ConsoleText.Text = joined
    if UI.LogsText then
        UI.LogsText.Text = joined
        pcall(function()
            local h = UI.LogsText.AbsoluteSize.Y + 16
            if UI.LogsScroll then
                UI.LogsScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, h)
                UI.LogsScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, h - UI.LogsScroll.AbsoluteSize.Y))
            end
        end)
    end
    scrollToBottom()
end

clearLog = function()
    logLines = {}
    local cleared = '<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">cleared</font>'
    ConsoleText.Text = cleared
    if UI.LogsText then UI.LogsText.Text = cleared end
    UI.Console.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    if UI.LogsScroll then UI.LogsScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end
end

if UI.LogsClearBtn then
    bindClick(UI.LogsClearBtn, function()
        clearLog()
        setStatus("logs cleared", LOG.dim)
    end)
end

setStatus = function(msg, color)
    logRich('<font color="' .. (color or LOG.dim) .. '">' .. tostring(msg) .. "</font>")
end

bumpSolvedLabel = function()
    UI.SolvedLabel.Text = _solvedCount .. " solved / " .. _askedCount .. " asked"
end

bindClick(UI.ClearBtn, function()
    clearLog()
    _solvedCount, _askedCount = 0, 0
    bumpSolvedLabel()
end)

-- power visual + header controls
applyPowerVisual = function()
    local on = cfg.sniper
    tw(Power, { BackgroundColor3 = on and HEADLESS_PURPLE or Theme.ToggleOff }, 0.12)
    tw(UI.PowerDot, { Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -14) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -14) }, 0.12)
    tw(UI.LiveDot, { BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff }, 0.12)
    if UI.ReadyDot then tw(UI.ReadyDot, { BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.Green or Theme.ToggleOff }, 0.12) end
    if UI.ReadyLabel then UI.ReadyLabel.Text = on and "READY" or "PAUSED"; UI.ReadyLabel.TextColor3 = on and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim end
    if UI.SubTitle and UI.SubTitle.Visible then
        UI.SubTitle.Text = on and "CODE SNIPER  •  LISTENING" or "CODE SNIPER  •  PAUSED"
        UI.SubTitle.TextColor3 = on and Theme.Green or Theme.Dim
    end
    tw(RedeemerBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(28, 90, 48) or Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32) }, 0.12)
    tw(UI.RedeemerDot, { BackgroundColor3 = on and ACTIVE_GREEN or Theme.ToggleOff }, 0.12)
    tw(UI.RedeemerStroke, { Color = on and ACTIVE_GREEN or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 55) }, 0.12)
    RedeemerState.Text = on and "ON" or "OFF"
    RedeemerState.TextColor3 = on and ACTIVE_GREEN or Theme.Dim
    tw(UI.WindowOutline, { Transparency = on and 0.08 or 0.45 }, 0.12)
    if setPowerRow then setPowerRow(on) end
end

local function toggleSniper()
    cfg.sniper = not cfg.sniper
    saveConfig()
    applyPowerVisual()
    if cfg.sniper then
        clearCapture()
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">sniper on</font>')
    else
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">sniper off</font>')
    end
end
bindClick(Power, toggleSniper)
bindClick(RedeemerBtn, toggleSniper)

local menuOpen = false
local function setMenuOpen(open)
    menuOpen = open
    if GUI then GUI.Enabled = true end
    if open then
        Window.Visible = true
        Window.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W, 38)
        Window.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        if MenuScale then MenuScale.Scale = (cfg.menuScale or 1) * 0.92 end
        twBounce(Window, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W, WIN_H), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.38)
        if MenuScale then twSoft(MenuScale, { Scale = cfg.menuScale or 1 }, 0.32) end
        MenuBtn.Text = "CLOSE"
        pcall(function()
            for _, d in ipairs(Window:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("GuiObject") and d.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then
                    local goal = d.BackgroundTransparency
                    d.BackgroundTransparency = math.min(1, goal + 0.35)
                    twSoft(d, { BackgroundTransparency = goal }, 0.28)
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        MenuBtn.Text = "MENU"
        if MenuScale then twSoft(MenuScale, { Scale = (cfg.menuScale or 1) * 0.9 }, 0.22) end
        twSoft(Window, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WIN_W, 38), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 }, 0.26)
        task.delay(0.27, function()
            if not menuOpen then
                Window.Visible = false
                Window.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                if MenuScale then MenuScale.Scale = cfg.menuScale or 1 end
            end
        end)
    end
end

bindClick(MenuBtn, function() setMenuOpen(not menuOpen) end)

bindClick(UI.CloseBtn, function()
    setMenuOpen(false)
end)

-- dragging (scale aware, like the hub's makeDraggable)
do
    local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil, nil
    local THRESHOLD = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 8 or 2
    local moved = false

    local function overControl(position)
        for _, control in ipairs({ Power, UI.MinBtn, UI.CloseBtn }) do
            local pos, size = control.AbsolutePosition, control.AbsoluteSize
            if position.X >= pos.X - 8 and position.X <= pos.X + size.X + 8
                and position.Y >= pos.Y - 8 and position.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y + 8 then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    UI.Header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if dragging or overControl(input.Position) then return end
        dragging, dragInput = true, input
        dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        startPos = Window.Position
        moved = false
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End
                or input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
                if input == dragInput then dragging, dragInput = false, nil end
            end
        end)
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging or not dragInput then return end
        local trackedTouch = dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == dragInput
        local trackedMouse = dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        if not trackedTouch and not trackedMouse then return end

        local delta = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y) - dragStart
        if not moved then
            if delta.Magnitude < THRESHOLD then return end
            moved = true
        end
        local scale = GlobalScale.Scale
        if scale <= 0 then scale = 1 end
        Window.Position = UDim2.new(
            startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + (delta.X / scale),
            startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + (delta.Y / scale)
        )
    end)
end

-- The compact launcher is draggable by one finger/mouse from empty space.
do
    local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil, nil
    local moved = false
    local THRESHOLD = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 8 or 2

    local function overLauncherControl(position)
        for _, control in ipairs({ RedeemerBtn, MenuBtn }) do
            local pos, size = control.AbsolutePosition, control.AbsoluteSize
            if position.X >= pos.X - 6 and position.X <= pos.X + size.X + 6
                and position.Y >= pos.Y - 6 and position.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y + 6 then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    Launcher.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if dragging or overLauncherControl(input.Position) then return end
        dragging, dragInput = true, input
        dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        startPos = Launcher.Position
        moved = false
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End
                or input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
                if input == dragInput then dragging, dragInput = false, nil end
            end
        end)
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging or not dragInput then return end
        local trackedTouch = dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == dragInput
        local trackedMouse = dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        if not trackedTouch and not trackedMouse then return end
        local delta = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y) - dragStart
        if not moved and delta.Magnitude < THRESHOLD then return end
        moved = true
        local scale = math.max(GlobalScale.Scale, 0.01)
        Launcher.Position = UDim2.new(
            startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X / scale,
            startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y / scale
        )
    end)
end

-- Keybinds
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    local key = input.KeyCode
    -- RightControl: hide/show entire GUI
    if key == Enum.KeyCode.RightControl then
        if GUI then GUI.Enabled = not GUI.Enabled end
        return
    end
    -- RightShift: toggle menu panel
    if key == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift then
        if type(setMenuOpen) == "function" then
            setMenuOpen(not menuOpen)
        end
        return
    end
    -- P: toggle sniper / power
    if key == Enum.KeyCode.P and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
        cfg.sniper = not cfg.sniper
        pcall(saveConfig)
        if type(applyPowerVisual) == "function" then pcall(applyPowerVisual) end
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">sniper </font><font color="'
            .. (cfg.sniper and LOG.ok or LOG.err) .. '">' .. (cfg.sniper and "on" or "off") .. " (P)</font>")
        return
    end
    -- R: force submit current code box
    if key == Enum.KeyCode.R and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
        local code = trim((UI.CodeBox and UI.CodeBox.Text) or "")
        if code == "" then code = trim(_lastNonBlankText or "") end
        if code ~= "" then
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, msg = typeAndSubmitCode(code)
                setStatus((ok and "HOTKEY R  •  " or "HOTKEY FAIL  •  ") .. code, ok and LOG.ok or LOG.err)
            end)
        end
        return
    end
end)

-- open animation, same as the hub's openAnim
do
    local target = Window.Position
    MenuScale.Scale = cfg.menuScale * 0.94
    Window.Position = UDim2.new(target.X.Scale, target.X.Offset, target.Y.Scale, target.Y.Offset + 18)
    tw(MenuScale, { Scale = cfg.menuScale }, 0.20)
    tw(Window, { Position = target }, 0.20)
end

-- HEADLESS intro: horseman runs across black screen, then "(we paid)"
-- Sound: set cfg.introSoundId = "rbxassetid://YOUR_ID" (must be an asset you have rights to)
local function playHeadlessIntro(done)
    -- Instant boot — skip heavy intro so UI always opens
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            if GUI then GUI.Enabled = true end
            if Launcher then Launcher.Visible = true end
        end)
        task.wait(0.05)
        if done then pcall(done) end
    end)
end

local _uiShown = false
local function showMainUI()
    if _uiShown then return end
    _uiShown = true
    pcall(function()
        if GUI then
            GUI.Enabled = true
            GUI.DisplayOrder = 9999999
            if not GUI.Parent then
                local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or playerGui
                if pg then GUI.Parent = pg end
            end
        end
        if Master then Master.Visible = true end
        if Launcher then
            Launcher.Visible = true
            Launcher.BackgroundTransparency = 0.01
            Launcher.ZIndex = 50
        end
        if UI.DebobCredit then UI.DebobCredit.Visible = true end
        if type(applyPowerVisual) == "function" then pcall(applyPowerVisual) end
        if type(applyTheme) == "function" then pcall(applyTheme) end
        if type(bumpSolvedLabel) == "function" then pcall(bumpSolvedLabel) end
    end)
    print("[HEADLESS HUB] showMainUI fired")
    pcall(function()
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.acc .. '">HEADLESS HUB REDEEMER ready</font>')
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">AI: lore memory + rethink on invalid</font>')
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">keys: RCtrl hide  •  RShift menu  •  P sniper  •  R submit</font>')
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">redeem delay </font><font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">'
            .. tostring(cfg.redeemDelay) .. "s</font><font color=\"" .. LOG.dim .. '\">  •  spam </font><font color="'
            .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. tostring(cfg.submitSpam) .. "</font><font color=\"" .. LOG.dim
            .. '\">  •  rethink </font><font color="' .. (cfg.rethinkInvalid and LOG.ok or LOG.err) .. '">'
            .. (cfg.rethinkInvalid and ("on x" .. tostring(cfg.rethinkMax)) or "off") .. "</font>")
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">waiting for codes / riddles...</font>')
    end)
    print("[HEADLESS HUB] UI ready — MENU / RightShift to open")
end

-- failsafes — UI MUST open
for _, sec in ipairs({ 0.3, 1, 2, 4, 8 }) do
    task.delay(sec, function()
        if not _uiShown then
            print("[HEADLESS HUB] failsafe " .. tostring(sec) .. "s — forcing UI")
            showMainUI()
        end
        pcall(function()
            if GUI then GUI.Enabled = true end
            if Launcher then Launcher.Visible = true end
        end)
    end)
end

pcall(playHeadlessIntro, showMainUI)
task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.15)
    if not _uiShown then showMainUI() end
end)

-- CODE CAPTURE + SUBMIT FLOW
clearCapture = function()
    _capturedParts = {}
    _submitBusy = false
    if UI and UI.CodeBox then
        pcall(function() UI.CodeBox.Text = "" end)
    end
end

-- Sammy said "use code" again / "o wait" → wipe fragments and re-listen
local function restartCodeCapture(reason)
    clearCapture()
    if type(_seen) == "table" then
        for k in pairs(_seen) do _seen[k] = nil end
    end
    if type(logRich) == "function" then
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.warn .. '">restart capture'
            .. (reason and (" • " .. tostring(reason)) or "") .. "</font>")
    end
    if type(setStatus) == "function" then
        setStatus("RESTART  •  listening for code…", LOG.warn)
    end
end

local function clearBoxWatchers()
    if _boxTextConn then pcall(function() _boxTextConn:Disconnect() end) end
    if _boxAncestryConn then pcall(function() _boxAncestryConn:Disconnect() end) end
    _boxTextConn, _boxAncestryConn, _lastWatchedBox = nil, nil, nil
end

local function watchBox(box)
    if not box or _lastWatchedBox == box then return end
    clearBoxWatchers()
    _lastWatchedBox = box
    if box.Text ~= "" then _lastNonBlankText = box.Text end
    _boxTextConn = box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if box.Text == "" then clearCapture() else _lastNonBlankText = box.Text end
    end)
    _boxAncestryConn = box.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then clearCapture() clearBoxWatchers() end
    end)
end

clearPending = function()
    _pendingToken += 1
    _pendingText, _pendingBox, _pendingUntil = nil, nil, 0
    _pendingFromRiddle = false
end

rememberPending = function(box, text, replaceExisting, fromRiddle)
    if not text or text == "" then return end
    if not replaceExisting and _pendingText and os.clock() <= _pendingUntil then return end
    _pendingToken += 1
    local token = _pendingToken
    _pendingText, _pendingBox = text, box
    -- preserve riddle flag when caller doesn't pass it (e.g. typeAndSubmitCode)
    if fromRiddle ~= nil then
        _pendingFromRiddle = fromRiddle == true
    end
    _pendingUntil = os.clock() + 8
    task.delay(8, function()
        if token == _pendingToken then clearPending() end
    end)
end

local _retypeBusy = false
local _retypeLeft = 0

local function spamSubmitCode(code)
    local ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(code)
    return ok, message
end

local function retryInvalidCode(box, code)
    if _retypeBusy then return end
    if not cfg.retypeInvalid or not code or code == "" then return end
    local times = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.retypeTimes) or 3), 1, 15)
    local speed = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.retypeSpeed) or 0, 0, 2.00)
    _retypeBusy = true
    _retypeLeft = times
    task.spawn(function()
        for attempt = 1, times do
            if not cfg.retypeInvalid then break end
            if speed > 0 then task.wait(speed) end
            _retypeLeft = times - attempt
            setStatus(
                "RETRY " .. tostring(attempt) .. "/" .. tostring(times)
                    .. "  •  " .. code
                    .. "  •  " .. string.format("%.2fs", speed),
                LOG.warn
            )
            rememberPending(box, code, true, false)
            local ok, message = spamSubmitCode(code)
            if not ok then
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">  retry failed: ' .. tostring(message) .. "</font>")
            else
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">  resubmitted x'
                    .. tostring(math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.submitSpam) or 1), 1, 10))
                    .. "</font>")
            end
        end
        _retypeBusy = false
        _retypeLeft = 0
    end)
end

local function restoreRejected(box, text)
    if not cfg.retypeInvalid or not text or text == "" then return false end
    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    local target = currentCodeBox() or box
    if not target or not isVisibleChain(target) then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function() target.Text = text end)
    if ok then
        _lastBox = target
        watchBox(target)
    end
    return ok
end

handleFeedback = function(text, sourceObject)
    if not _pendingText then return end
    if os.clock() > _pendingUntil then clearPending() return end
    if sourceObject and sourceObject:IsDescendantOf(GUI) then return end
    local lower = tostring(text or ""):lower()
    local rejected = lower:find("invalid", 1, true)
        or lower:find("expired", 1, true)
        or lower:find("already redeemed", 1, true)
        or lower:find("already used", 1, true)
        or lower:find("doesn't exist", 1, true)
        or lower:find("does not exist", 1, true)
        or lower:find("not found", 1, true)
        or lower:find("rejected", 1, true)
        or lower:find("wrong code", 1, true)
        or lower:find("code failed", 1, true)
        or lower:find("try again", 1, true)
        or lower:find("incorrect", 1, true)
        or lower:find("not valid", 1, true)
        or lower:find("fail", 1, true)
        or lower:find("error", 1, true)
    local accepted = lower:find("code redeemed", 1, true)
        or lower:find("redeemed successfully", 1, true)
        or lower:find("successfully redeemed", 1, true)
        or lower:find("code accepted", 1, true)
        or lower:find("reward claimed", 1, true)
        or lower:find("code claimed", 1, true)
        or lower:find("success", 1, true)
        or lower == "redeemed"
    if not rejected and not accepted then return end

    local previousText, previousBox = _pendingText, _pendingBox
    local wasRiddle = _pendingFromRiddle
    if accepted and not rejected then
        clearPending()
        _retypeBusy = false
        setStatus("VALID  •  " .. previousText, LOG.ok)
        -- Discord webhook: announce what was redeemed (+ brainrot image when known)
        task.spawn(function()
            local reward = tostring(text or "")
            local name = reward:match("[Rr]edeemed%s+([%w%s%-%_]+)")
                or reward:match("[Gg]ot%s+([%w%s%-%_]+)")
                or reward:match("[Rr]eceived%s+([%w%s%-%_]+)")
                or reward:match("[Uu]nlocked%s+([%w%s%-%_]+)")
                or previousText
            name = tostring(name or previousText):gsub("%s+", " "):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            if name == "" then name = previousText end
            local upperName = string.upper(name)
            local content = "**" .. upperName .. " REDEEMED**"

            local BRAINROT_IMAGES = {
                ["JOHN PORK"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/d/d2/John_Pork.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/1000?cb=20260502233229",
                ["JOHNPORK"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/d/d2/John_Pork.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/1000?cb=20260502233229",
                ["MEOWL"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/6/62/Meowl.png/revision/latest?cb=20251022133154",
                ["STRAWBERRY ELEPHANT"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/7/7b/Strawberry_Elephant.png/revision/latest?cb=20261010220206",
                ["STRAWBERRYELEPHANT"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/7/7b/Strawberry_Elephant.png/revision/latest?cb=20261010220206",
                ["SKIBIDI TOILET"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/a/a7/Default_Skibidi_Toilet.png/revision/latest?cb=20260528092806",
                ["SKIBIDITOILET"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/a/a7/Default_Skibidi_Toilet.png/revision/latest?cb=20260528092806",
                ["DRAGON CANNELLONI"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/a/a5/Dragon_Cannelloni.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/1000?cb=20260428162417",
                ["DRAGONCANNELLONI"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/a/a5/Dragon_Cannelloni.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/1000?cb=20260428162417",
                ["DRAGON SKELETONINI"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/5/51/Dragon_Skeletonini.png/revision/latest?cb=20261010204212",
                ["DRAGONSKELETONINI"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/5/51/Dragon_Skeletonini.png/revision/latest?cb=20261010204212",
                ["PIZZA AND RANCH"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/2/21/Pizza_and_Ranch.png/revision/latest?cb=20260726021649",
                ["PIZZAANDRANCH"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/2/21/Pizza_and_Ranch.png/revision/latest?cb=20260726021649",
                ["PHOENIX"] = "https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/stealabr/images/c/c1/Phoenix.png/revision/latest?cb=20261004215622",
            }
            local img = BRAINROT_IMAGES[upperName] or BRAINROT_IMAGES[upperName:gsub("%s+", "")]
            if not img then
                for key, u in pairs(BRAINROT_IMAGES) do
                    if upperName:find(key, 1, true) or key:find(upperName, 1, true) then
                        img = u
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            pcall(function()
                local url = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1558620517190074469/oYd02B1NfyVJ428lln5g--RyTsBjIVgh4vG6FONY0gcUWG_tFUgS3SO2KRTTDnbwOiVP"
                local body = { content = content, username = "Headless Hub" }
                if img then
                    body.embeds = {{
                        title = upperName .. " REDEEMED",
                        color = 16744448,
                        image = { url = img },
                    }}
                end
                local payload = HttpService:JSONEncode(body)
                local headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }
                if typeof(request) == "function" then
                    request({ Url = url, Method = "POST", Headers = headers, Body = payload })
                elseif typeof(http_request) == "function" then
                    http_request({ Url = url, Method = "POST", Headers = headers, Body = payload })
                elseif typeof(syn) == "table" and typeof(syn.request) == "function" then
                    syn.request({ Url = url, Method = "POST", Headers = headers, Body = payload })
                elseif typeof(fluxus) == "table" and typeof(fluxus.request) == "function" then
                    fluxus.request({ Url = url, Method = "POST", Headers = headers, Body = payload })
                end
            end)
            logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">webhook: ' .. content .. (img and " +image" or "") .. "</font>")
        end)
        return
    end

    -- mark answer as rejected so AI / memory avoid it
    if type(memoryReject) == "function" then
        pcall(memoryReject, previousText)
    end

    clearPending()

    -- RETHINK: if this came from a riddle solve, ask AI for a different answer
    if wasRiddle and cfg.rethinkInvalid and _lastRiddleQ and cfg.riddleSolver then
        local qKey = basicClean(_lastRiddleQ)
        local ansKey = upperClean(previousText)
        local tries = _rethinkTried[qKey] or 0
        local maxTry = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.rethinkMax) or 2), 1, 5)
        if tries < maxTry then
            _rethinkTried[qKey] = tries + 1
            if type(memoryReject) == "function" then pcall(memoryReject, previousText) end
            setStatus("INVALID  •  " .. previousText .. "  → RETHINK " .. (tries + 1) .. "/" .. maxTry, LOG.warn)
            logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.warn .. '">rethink: asking AI for alternate (reject '
                .. tostring(previousText) .. ")...</font>")
            task.spawn(function()
                local alt, note = solveRiddleWithApi(_lastRiddleQ, true, previousText)
                if alt and alt ~= "" and upperClean(alt) ~= ansKey then
                    _lastRiddleA = alt
                    if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = alt end
                    logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">  rethink answer: </font><font color="'
                        .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. alt .. '</font>')
                    if cfg.autoSubmit then
                        rememberPending(previousBox, alt, true, true)
                        local ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(alt)
                        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">  rethink submit: </font><font color="'
                            .. (ok and LOG.ok or LOG.err) .. '">' .. tostring(message) .. "</font>")
                    else
                        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">  auto submit off — use answer manually</font>')
                    end
                else
                    logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">  rethink failed: ' .. tostring(note or "no alternate") .. "</font>")
                    if cfg.retypeInvalid and not _retypeBusy then
                        restoreRejected(previousBox, previousText)
                        retryInvalidCode(previousBox, previousText)
                    end
                end
            end)
            return
        end
    end

    -- invalid: retype ONLY if toggle is on
    if cfg.retypeInvalid and not _retypeBusy then
        restoreRejected(previousBox, previousText)
        retryInvalidCode(previousBox, previousText)
        setStatus("INVALID  •  " .. previousText .. "  (auto retry on)", LOG.warn)
    else
        setStatus("INVALID  •  " .. previousText, LOG.err)
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">invalid — retype off, no resubmit</font>')
    end
end

-- early blocklist (must exist before appendToBox / extractCodes)
if not isBlockedToken then
    BLOCKED_TOKENS = BLOCKED_TOKENS or {
        setcreatorid = true, setcreator = true, creatorid = true,
        creator_id = true, setuserid = true, setuser = true,
    }
    function isBlockedToken(s)
        local t = tostring(s or ""):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w_]", "")
        if t == "" then return true end
        if BLOCKED_TOKENS[t] then return true end
        if t:find("setcreator", 1, true) then return true end
        if t:find("creatorid", 1, true) then return true end
        if t:find("locked", 1, true) and t:find("base", 1, true) then return true end
        return false
    end
end

local _submitBusy = false

appendToBox = function(text)
    if not text or text == "" then return end
    if isBlockedToken(text) then return end
    -- never treat pure trigger words as code fragments
    local tl = tostring(text):lower()
    if tl == "use" or tl == "code" or tl == "codes" or tl == "redeem" or tl == "then" then
        return
    end
    if _lastWatchedBox and not isVisibleChain(_lastWatchedBox) then
        clearCapture()
        clearBoxWatchers()
    end

    -- queue word if a submit is mid-flight (don't drop it)
    if _submitBusy and cfg.spamRedeem ~= true then
        task.delay(0.05, function()
            if not _submitBusy then appendToBox(text) end
        end)
        return
    end

    local box = currentCodeBox()
    _capturedParts[#_capturedParts + 1] = text
    local combined = table.concat(_capturedParts)
    local count = #_capturedParts
    local need = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.submitAfter) or 1), 1, 4)

    if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = combined end
    if box then
        _lastBox = box
        watchBox(box)
        pcall(function() box.Text = combined end)
    end

    logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">code ' .. count .. "/" .. need
        .. ': </font><font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. combined .. "</font>")

    local function doSubmit(toSend, label)
        cfg.autoSubmit = true
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local pg = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                if not pg then return end
                local codes = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
                if codes and codes:IsA("ScreenGui") then codes.Enabled = true end
                if type(findCodeButtons) == "function" then
                    for _, btn in ipairs(findCodeButtons(pg)) do clickButton(btn) end
                end
            end)
            rememberPending(nil, toSend, true, false)
            local ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(toSend)
            if ok then
                setStatus("SUBMITTED  •  " .. toSend .. (label or ""), LOG.white)
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">auto submit: ' .. toSend .. (label or "") .. "</font>")
            else
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">submit failed: ' .. tostring(message) .. "</font>")
            end
        end)
    end

    -- SPAM REDEEM ON → spam-click submit on EVERY word
    if cfg.spamRedeem == true then
        doSubmit(combined, " [spam word " .. count .. "]")
    end

    -- Normal threshold submit (always when count hits need)
    if count >= need then
        local toSend = combined
        _capturedParts = {} -- reset for next code
        if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = toSend end
        if cfg.spamRedeem ~= true then
            -- single clean submit only when spam is OFF
            _submitBusy = true
            task.spawn(function()
                doSubmit(toSend, "")
                task.wait(0.04)
                _submitBusy = false
            end)
        else
            -- already spamming each word; final clear
            doSubmit(toSend, " [final]")
        end
    end
end

-- RIDDLE QUEUE
-- Animated status: "TEXT." → "TEXT.." → ... up to maxDots, then shrink back
local _statusAnimToken = 0
local function animateStatusDots(baseText, color, maxDots, holdLoops)
    maxDots = maxDots or 5
    holdLoops = holdLoops or 1
    _statusAnimToken += 1
    local token = _statusAnimToken
    task.spawn(function()
        -- grow 1 → maxDots
        for d = 1, maxDots do
            if token ~= _statusAnimToken then return end
            setStatus(baseText .. string.rep(".", d), color or LOG.dim)
            task.wait(0.28)
        end
        for _ = 1, holdLoops do
            if token ~= _statusAnimToken then return end
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        -- shrink maxDots → 1
        for d = maxDots - 1, 1, -1 do
            if token ~= _statusAnimToken then return end
            setStatus(baseText .. string.rep(".", d), color or LOG.dim)
            task.wait(0.28)
        end
        -- loop once more briefly if still same token
        if token == _statusAnimToken then
            for d = 2, 3 do
                if token ~= _statusAnimToken then return end
                setStatus(baseText .. string.rep(".", d), color or LOG.dim)
                task.wait(0.28)
            end
        end
    end)
    return token
end

local function stopStatusAnim()
    _statusAnimToken += 1
end

local function runRiddle(question)
    _askedCount += 1
    bumpSolvedLabel()

    -- if the "riddle" is actually a statement, learn it for later
    pcall(memoryLearnFromSpeech, question)

    logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">riddle: </font>'
        .. '<font color="' .. LOG.white .. '">' .. question:sub(1, 90) .. "</font>")

    local answer, breakdown, note
    _lastRiddleQ = question
    _lastRiddleA = nil

    -- Local smart solve first (instant), then Groq AI
    local localAns, localSrc = solveRiddleOffline(question)
    if localAns and localAns ~= "" then
        answer = localAns
        breakdown = { (localSrc or "local") .. " = " .. localAns }
        note = localSrc or "local"
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">local solve hit</font>')
    else
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">asking AI...</font>')
        animateStatusDots("AI IS THINKING", LOG.warn, 5, 1)
        local aiAnswer, aiNote = solveRiddleWithApi(question)
        stopStatusAnim()
        if aiAnswer and aiAnswer ~= "" then
            answer = aiAnswer
            breakdown = { "AI = " .. aiAnswer }
            note = "AI"
        else
            logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.warn .. '">  AI failed</font>')
            note = "AI failed"
            -- ANSWER UNKNOWN... animate 3→5 dots then shrink 1 by 1
            task.spawn(function()
                local token
                _statusAnimToken += 1
                token = _statusAnimToken
                -- start at 3 dots, grow to 5
                for d = 3, 5 do
                    if token ~= _statusAnimToken then return end
                    setStatus("ANSWER UNKNOWN" .. string.rep(".", d), LOG.err)
                    task.wait(0.32)
                end
                -- shrink 5 → 1
                for d = 4, 1, -1 do
                    if token ~= _statusAnimToken then return end
                    setStatus("ANSWER UNKNOWN" .. string.rep(".", d), LOG.err)
                    task.wait(0.32)
                end
                if token == _statusAnimToken then
                    setStatus("ANSWER UNKNOWN...", LOG.err)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    if breakdown then
        for _, line in ipairs(breakdown) do
            local colour = line:sub(-1) == "?" and LOG.err or LOG.dim
            logRich('<font color="' .. colour .. '">  ' .. line .. "</font>")
        end
    end

    if not answer or answer == "" then
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">  ' .. tostring(note or "not in database") .. "</font>")
        return
    end

    _solvedCount += 1
    bumpSolvedLabel()
    _lastRiddleA = answer
    logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">  answer: </font><font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. answer .. "</font>")
    if note then
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.warn .. '">  ' .. note .. "</font>")
    end

    -- Always show answer in hub box
    if UI.CodeBox then
        UI.CodeBox.Text = answer
    end
    setStatus("RIDDLE  •  " .. answer, LOG.ok)

    -- ALWAYS auto-submit riddle answers instantly (0 delay)
    cfg.autoSubmit = true
    local toSend = answer
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            local pg = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            if not pg then return end
            local codes = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
            if codes and codes:IsA("ScreenGui") then codes.Enabled = true end
            if type(findCodeButtons) == "function" then
                for _, btn in ipairs(findCodeButtons(pg)) do clickButton(btn) end
            end
        end)
        local box = currentCodeBox()
        if box then pcall(function() box.Text = toSend end) end
        local oldDelay = cfg.redeemDelay
        cfg.redeemDelay = 0
        rememberPending(box, toSend, true, true)
        local ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(toSend)
        cfg.redeemDelay = oldDelay
        logRich('<font color="' .. (ok and LOG.ok or LOG.err) .. '">  riddle submit: '
            .. (ok and tostring(message) or ("failed: " .. tostring(message))) .. "</font>")
        if ok then
            setStatus("SUBMITTED  •  " .. toSend .. "  (riddle)", LOG.ok)
        else
            ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(toSend)
            if ok then
                setStatus("SUBMITTED  •  " .. toSend .. "  (riddle retry)", LOG.ok)
            else
                setStatus("FAILED  •  " .. toSend .. "  (" .. tostring(message) .. ")", LOG.err)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function pumpQueue()
    if _riddleBusy then return end
    _riddleBusy = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while #_riddleQueue > 0 do
            local question = table.remove(_riddleQueue, 1)
            pcall(runRiddle, question)
        end
        _riddleBusy = false
    end)
end

local function queueRiddle(question)
    if #_riddleQueue > 6 then return end
    _riddleQueue[#_riddleQueue + 1] = question
    pumpQueue()
end

local function manualRedeemFromMenu()
    local code = trim(UI.CodeBox.Text)
    if code == "" then
        setStatus("type a code first", LOG.warn)
        return
    end

    local ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(code)
    if ok then
        setStatus("SUBMITTED  •  " .. code .. "  (checking...)", LOG.white)
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">manual redeem: </font><font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">' .. code .. "</font>")
    else
        setStatus("FAILED  •  " .. code .. "  (" .. tostring(message) .. ")", LOG.err)
    end
end

bindClick(UI.CodeRedeemBtn, manualRedeemFromMenu)
UI.CodeBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
    if enterPressed then manualRedeemFromMenu() end
end)

bindClick(UI.AskBtn, function()
    local question = trim(UI.AskBox.Text)
    if question == "" then
        setStatus("type a riddle first", LOG.warn)
        return
    end
    UI.AskBox.Text = ""
    queueRiddle(question)
end)
UI.AskBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
    if not enterPressed then return end
    local question = trim(UI.AskBox.Text)
    if question == "" then return end
    UI.AskBox.Text = ""
    queueRiddle(question)
end)

-- ANNOUNCEMENT LISTENER
local function tokenize(text)
    local words = {}
    -- keep alphanumeric + underscore + hyphen tokens
    for word in tostring(text or ""):gmatch("[%w_%-]+") do
        words[#words + 1] = word
    end
    return words
end

local _lastRiddleKey, _lastRiddleAt = "", 0

local CODE_TRIGGER_PHRASES = {
    "code is", "the code is", "codes is", "codes are", "code are",
    "code:", "code :", "codes:", "code =", "codes =", "code=", "codes=",
    "use code", "use the code", "enter code", "enter the code", "type code",
    "type the code", "redeem code", "redeem the code", "new code", "code drop",
    "code time", "here is the code", "heres the code", "here's the code",
    "code for", "active code", "working code", "today code", "todays code",
    "code right now", "quick code", "free code", "admin code",
    "use code..", "code is..", "codes is..", "say code", "the codes are",
    "code goes", "code will be", "the code", "a code", "this code",
    "put code", "put the code", "go redeem", "go type", "spam code",
    "code chat", "in chat", "type this", "redeem this", "use this",
    "codesare", "codeis", "usecode", -- glued variants
}

local function textHasCodeTrigger(text)
    local l = tostring(text or ""):lower()
    -- normalize spaced punctuation: "code . ." / "code..."
    local n = l:gsub("[%.,!%?]+", " "):gsub("%s+", " ")
    for _, phrase in ipairs(CODE_TRIGGER_PHRASES) do
        if l:find(phrase, 1, true) or n:find(phrase, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    -- flexible: any "code" near "is/are/use/redeem/type/enter"
    if n:find("code", 1, true) then
        if n:find("is", 1, true) or n:find("are", 1, true) or n:find("use", 1, true)
            or n:find("redeem", 1, true) or n:find("type", 1, true)
            or n:find("enter", 1, true) or n:find("drop", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local INVALID_CODE_TOKENS = {
    code = true, codes = true, use = true, redeem = true, enter = true,
    type = true, the = true, a = true, an = true, is = true, are = true,
    here = true, now = true, new = true, free = true, admin = true,
    drop = true, time = true, ["for"] = true, any = true, from = true,
    this = true, that = true, will = true, be = true, goes = true,
    chat = true, put = true, go = true, spam = true, right = true,
}

local function isPlausibleCode(code)
    if not code or code == "" then return false end
    local cl = code:lower()
    if INVALID_CODE_TOKENS[cl] or isBlockedToken(code) then return false end
    -- pure numbers 1-16 digits
    if code:match("^%d+$") and #code >= 1 and #code <= 16 then return true end
    -- mixed / alpha codes 2-40 chars
    if #code >= 2 and #code <= 40 and code:match("^[%w_%-]+$") then return true end
    return false
end

local function extractCodesFromPhrase(text)
    local found, seen = {}, {}
    local l = tostring(text or "")
    local function add(code)
        if not code then return end
        -- strip trailing punctuation leftovers
        code = code:gsub("^[%.:%-%s]+", ""):gsub("[%.:%-%s]+$", "")
        if not isPlausibleCode(code) then return end
        local key = code:lower()
        if seen[key] then return end
        seen[key] = true
        found[#found + 1] = code
    end

    -- Flexible patterns — optional dots/colons/dashes/equals between words
    local patterns = {
        -- code is X / code: X / code = X
        "[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee][sS]?%s*[Ii][Ss]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee][sS]?%s*[Aa][Rr][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee][sS]?%s*[%.:%=]+%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        -- use / redeem / enter / type code X
        "[Uu][Ss][Ee]%s+[Tt][Hh][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Uu][Ss][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Rr][Ee][Dd][Ee][Ee][Mm]%s+[Tt][Hh][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Rr][Ee][Dd][Ee][Ee][Mm]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Ee][Nn][Tt][Ee][Rr]%s+[Tt][Hh][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Ee][Nn][Tt][Ee][Rr]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Tt][Yy][Pp][Ee]%s+[Tt][Hh][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Tt][Yy][Pp][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*[%.:%-=]*%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        -- glued forms: usecodesomething / codeisSOMETHING
        "[Uu][Ss][Ee][Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee][Ii][Ss]%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        "[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee][Ss][Aa][Rr][Ee]%s*([%w_%-]+)",
        -- "the code X" / "code X" near end
        "[Tt][Hh][Ee]%s+[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]%s+([%w_%-][%w_%-]+)",
        -- last token after any code trigger word
        "[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee][sS]?[^%w\n]{0,16}([%w_%-][%w_%-][%w_%-]+)",
    }
    for _, pat in ipairs(patterns) do
        for code in l:gmatch(pat) do
            add(code)
        end
    end

    -- If still nothing: take last plausible token after the word "code"
    if #found == 0 then
        local lower = l:lower()
        local idx = lower:find("code", 1, true)
        if idx then
            local after = l:sub(idx + 4)
            for token in after:gmatch("[%w_%-]+") do
                if isPlausibleCode(token) then
                    add(token)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return found
end

local function autoEnableFromAnnouncement(text)
    if not cfg.autoListen then return false end
    if not textHasCodeTrigger(text) then return false end
    local wasOff = not cfg.sniper
    cfg.sniper = true
    pcall(saveConfig)
    if type(applyPowerVisual) == "function" then
        pcall(applyPowerVisual)
    end
    -- always try to open codes panel when a code phrase is heard
    pcall(function()
        local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end
        local codes = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
        if codes and codes:IsA("ScreenGui") then codes.Enabled = true end
        if type(findCodeButtons) == "function" then
            for _, btn in ipairs(findCodeButtons(pg)) do clickButton(btn) end
        end
    end)
    if wasOff and type(logRich) == "function" then
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">auto listen • sniper ON (heard code phrase)</font>')
    end
    return true
end

local SYSTEM_PHRASE_PATTERNS = {
    "joined the experience", "left the experience", "has joined", "has left",
    "friend request", "you got a friend", "badge awarded",
    "server hopping", "teleporting", "teleport failed",
    "loading", "please wait", "reconnect",
    "purchase complete", "purchase failed", "not enough",
    "premium", "robux", "gamepass",
    "voted", "vote kick", "kicked from",
    "chat filtered", "message filtered", "your message",
    "system:", "[system]", "(system)",
    "announcement:", "server:", "[server]",
    "tips:", "tip:", "hint:",
    "welcome to", "thanks for playing",
    "rate limit", "try again later",
    "error code", "http error",
    "rbxassetid",
    "setcreatorid", "setcreator", "creatorid", "creator_id",
    "you locked", "locked your base", "locked for",
}

local BLOCKED_TOKENS = {
    setcreatorid = true, setcreator = true, creatorid = true,
    creator_id = true, setuserid = true, setuser = true,
}

local function isBlockedToken(s)
    local t = tostring(s or ""):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("[^%w_]", "")
    if t == "" then return true end
    if BLOCKED_TOKENS[t] then return true end
    if t:find("setcreator", 1, true) then return true end
    if t:find("creatorid", 1, true) then return true end
    if t:find("locked", 1, true) and t:find("base", 1, true) then return true end
    return false
end

local function isSystemPhrase(msg)
    if isBlockedToken(msg) then return true end
    if cfg.ignoreSystemPhrases == false then return false end
    local l = tostring(msg or ""):lower()
    if l == "" then return true end
    -- allow pure number codes like "67" / "1234" — do NOT treat as system noise
    local compactCheck = l:gsub("%s+", "")
    if compactCheck:match("^%d+$") and #compactCheck >= 1 and #compactCheck <= 12 then
        return false
    end
    if not l:find("%a") and not compactCheck:match("^%d+$") then return true end
    for _, pat in ipairs(SYSTEM_PHRASE_PATTERNS) do
        if l:find(pat, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    -- common bot / automated prefixes
    if l:sub(1, 1) == "[" and l:find("]", 1, true) then
        local tag = l:match("^%[(.-)%]")
        if tag and (#tag <= 12) and (
            tag:find("sys", 1, true) or tag:find("bot", 1, true)
            or tag:find("server", 1, true) or tag:find("admin", 1, true)
            or tag:find("auto", 1, true)
        ) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function onAnnouncement(...)
    local text = trim(stripRich(tostring((...) or "")))
    if text == "" then return end
    if isSystemPhrase(text) then return end
    -- skip pure code-like single tokens (e.g. setcreatorid)
    local compact = text:gsub("%s+", "")
    if #text < 40 and not text:find(" ") and compact:match("^[%w_%-]+$") and #compact >= 6 then
        -- still allow code triggers below, but do not learn as speech
    else
        if type(memoryLearnFromSpeech) == "function" then
            pcall(memoryLearnFromSpeech, text)
        end
    end

    -- Always check auto-listen triggers first
    autoEnableFromAnnouncement(text)
    if not cfg.sniper then return end

    local lower = text:lower()

    -- Riddles: always handle question-like announcements (don't require toggle for basic solve)
    do
        local looks = (type(looksLikeRiddle) == "function" and looksLikeRiddle(text)) or false
        local everything = cfg.answerEverything == true
        local isQ = lower:find("?", 1, true)
            or lower:find("riddle", 1, true)
            or lower:match("^what")
            or lower:match("^who")
            or lower:match("^how")
            or lower:match("^where")
            or lower:match("^when")
            or lower:match("^which")
            or lower:match("^name")
            or lower:match("^tell")
            or lower:match("^spell")
            or lower:match("^guess")
            or lower:match("^say")

        -- pure "use code X" is NOT a riddle
        local pulledEarly = extractCodesFromPhrase(text)
        if #pulledEarly > 0 and not isQ and not lower:find("riddle", 1, true) then
            -- fall through to code path below
        elseif (looks or isQ or everything) and (cfg.riddleSolver ~= false) then
            if everything then
                if isBlockedToken(text) then return end
                local words = 0
                for _ in lower:gmatch("%S+") do words = words + 1 end
                if words < 1 then return end
                if words == 1 and #compact >= 6 and compact:match("^[%w_%-]+$") then
                    return
                end
            end

            local key = basicClean(text)
            local cooldown = everything and 2.5 or 3
            if key == _lastRiddleKey and tick() - _lastRiddleAt < cooldown then return end
            _lastRiddleKey, _lastRiddleAt = key, tick()
            cfg.autoSubmit = true
            queueRiddle(text)
            return
        end
    end

    -- ANY alphanumeric token is a valid fragment (any word, any number)
    local function isValidCodeToken(code)
        if not code or code == "" then return false end
        if isBlockedToken(code) then return false end
        if code:match("^%d+$") and #code >= 1 and #code <= 16 then return true end
        if #code >= 1 and code:match("^[%w_%-]+$") then return true end
        return false
    end

    local TRIGGER_ONLY = {
        code = true, codes = true, use = true, redeem = true,
        enter = true, type = true, the = true, a = true, an = true,
        is = true, are = true, here = true, now = true, new = true,
        free = true, drop = true, time = true, any = true, from = true,
    }

    -- Restart cues: "o wait", "wait", "nevermind", bare "use code.", etc.
    do
        local compact = lower:gsub("[%p]+", " "):gsub("%s+", " "):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        local isRestart =
            compact == "o wait" or compact == "oh wait" or compact == "wait"
            or compact:find("^o wait ") == 1 or compact:find("^oh wait ") == 1
            or compact == "nevermind" or compact == "never mind" or compact == "hold on"
            or compact == "stop" or compact == "restart" or compact == "enter"
            or compact == "use code" or compact == "use the code"
            or compact == "code is" or compact == "codes are"
            or compact == "redeem code" or compact == "type code"
            or compact == "use code enter" or compact == "code enter"
        if isRestart then
            restartCodeCapture(compact)
            -- if "o wait ECLIPSE" — after restart, do NOT treat ECLIPSE as a fragment
            return
        end
    end

    -- "use code ..." / "code is ..."
    if textHasCodeTrigger(text) then
        local pulled = extractCodesFromPhrase(text)
        if #pulled > 0 then
            -- new full code after a previous partial → clear old fragments first
            restartCodeCapture("new full code")
            for _, code in ipairs(pulled) do
                local cl = code:lower()
                if not TRIGGER_ONLY[cl] and isValidCodeToken(code) then
                    local key = "full:" .. code
                    if not _seen[key] then
                        _seen[key] = true
                        task.delay(0.25, function() _seen[key] = nil end)
                        cfg.autoSubmit = true
                        task.spawn(function()
                            if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = code end
                            typeAndSubmitCode(code)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            return
        end

        -- bare trigger ("use code." with no token) → restart listening
        restartCodeCapture("use code (empty)")

        -- RIDDLE CODE: "use code my favorite color and favorite brainrot plus 67"
        -- Strip the trigger phrase and solve the rest as a multi-part riddle → one code
        local riddlePart = lower
        riddlePart = riddlePart
            :gsub("^.-use%s+the%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-use%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-redeem%s+the%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-redeem%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-enter%s+the%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-enter%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-type%s+the%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-type%s+code%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-the%s+code%s+is%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-code%s+is%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-codes%s+are%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-codes%s+is%s*[%.:%-]*%s*", "")
            :gsub("^.-code%s*:%s*", "")
        riddlePart = trim(riddlePart)
        if riddlePart ~= "" and #riddlePart >= 3 then
            logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">code riddle: </font><font color="'
                .. LOG.white .. '">' .. riddlePart:sub(1, 80) .. "</font>")
            local ans, src = nil, nil
            if type(smartLocalSolve) == "function" then
                ans, src = smartLocalSolve(riddlePart)
            end
            if (not ans or ans == "") and type(solveRiddleOffline) == "function" then
                ans, src = solveRiddleOffline(riddlePart)
            end
            if (not ans or ans == "") and type(solveRiddleWithApi) == "function" then
                ans, src = solveRiddleWithApi(riddlePart)
            end
            if ans and ans ~= "" then
                ans = tostring(ans):upper():gsub("%s+", "")
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">code riddle solve: ' .. ans
                    .. (src and (" (" .. tostring(src) .. ")") or "") .. "</font>")
                if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = ans end
                setStatus("RIDDLE CODE  •  " .. ans, LOG.ok)
                cfg.autoSubmit = true
                -- 0 delay submit
                task.spawn(function()
                    local oldDelay = cfg.redeemDelay
                    cfg.redeemDelay = 0
                    rememberPending(nil, ans, true, true)
                    local ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(ans)
                    cfg.redeemDelay = oldDelay
                    if ok then
                        setStatus("SUBMITTED  •  " .. ans .. "  (riddle code)", LOG.ok)
                        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">instant submit: ' .. ans .. "</font>")
                    else
                        -- one instant retry
                        ok, message = typeAndSubmitCode(ans)
                        logRich('<font color="' .. (ok and LOG.ok or LOG.err) .. '">riddle code: '
                            .. (ok and "ok" or tostring(message)) .. "</font>")
                    end
                end)
            else
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.warn .. '">code riddle unsolved</font>')
            end
        end
        return
    end

    -- Skip pure riddle questions (handled above)
    if lower:find("?", 1, true) or lower:find("riddle", 1, true)
        or lower:match("^what") or lower:match("^who") or lower:match("^how") then
        return
    end

    -- Entire announcement is one solid code token → instant submit
    do
        local only = text:gsub("[%s%p]+", "")
        if only ~= "" and isValidCodeToken(only) and not TRIGGER_ONLY[only:lower()] then
            local isSolid = (#only >= 5 and only:match("%d") and only:match("%a"))
                or (#only >= 8)
            if isSolid and not _seen["full:" .. only:lower()] then
                _seen["full:" .. only:lower()] = true
                task.delay(0.2, function() _seen["full:" .. only:lower()] = nil end)
                logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">full code: ' .. only .. "</font>")
                if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = only end
                task.spawn(function()
                    rememberPending(nil, only, true, false)
                    typeAndSubmitCode(only)
                end)
                return
            end
        end
    end

    -- Capture words as fragments for SUBMIT AFTER 1-4
    for _, word in ipairs(tokenize(text)) do
        local wl = word:lower()
        if not TRIGGER_ONLY[wl] and isValidCodeToken(word) then
            local dedupe = word:lower()
            if not _seen[dedupe] then
                _seen[dedupe] = true
                task.delay(0.12, function() _seen[dedupe] = nil end)
                cfg.autoSubmit = true
                local isSolid = (#word >= 5 and word:match("%d") and word:match("%a"))
                    or (#word >= 8 and word:match("^[%w_%-]+$"))
                if isSolid then
                    logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">full code: ' .. word .. "</font>")
                    if UI.CodeBox then UI.CodeBox.Text = word end
                    task.spawn(function()
                        rememberPending(nil, word, true, false)
                        typeAndSubmitCode(word)
                    end)
                else
                    appendToBox(word)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- two independent ways of finding the notification remote
local function resolveNotifyRemote()
    if _G.PhiNotifyRemote then return _G.PhiNotifyRemote end

    -- 1) require the NotificationController and read its upvalues
    local ok, controller = pcall(function()
        if not ReplicatedStorage then return nil end
        local controllers = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
        local notification = controllers and controllers:FindFirstChild("NotificationController", true)
        if notification then return require(notification) end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and type(controller) == "table" and type(controller.Start) == "function"
        and typeof(getupvalues) == "function" then
        local valuesOk, values = pcall(getupvalues, controller.Start)
        if valuesOk and type(values) == "table" then
            for _, value in pairs(values) do
                if typeof(value) == "Instance"
                    and (value:IsA("RemoteEvent") or value:IsA("UnreliableRemoteEvent")) then
                    return value
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- 2) scan Net remotes for a connection owned by NotificationController
    local getinfo = debug and (debug.getinfo or debug.info)
    local packages = ReplicatedStorage and ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
    local net = packages and packages:FindFirstChild("Net")
    if net and getinfo and getconns then
        for _, d in ipairs(net:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                local okConns, connections = pcall(getconns, d.OnClientEvent)
                if okConns and type(connections) == "table" then
                    for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
                        local fnOk, fn = pcall(function() return connection.Function end)
                        if fnOk and type(fn) == "function" then
                            local infoOk, info = pcall(getinfo, fn)
                            if infoOk and info
                                and tostring(info.short_src or info.source or ""):find("NotificationController", 1, true) then
                                return d
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local listenConn
task.spawn(function()
    local remote
    for attempt = 1, 25 do
        remote = resolveNotifyRemote()
        if remote and (remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or remote:IsA("UnreliableRemoteEvent")) then
            break
        end
        task.wait(0.4)
    end
    if remote and (remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") or remote:IsA("UnreliableRemoteEvent")) then
        if listenConn then pcall(function() listenConn:Disconnect() end) end
        listenConn = remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
            pcall(onAnnouncement, ...)
        end)
        if UI.LiveDot then UI.LiveDot.BackgroundColor3 = T.ok end
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.ok .. '">listener attached: ' .. remote.Name .. "</font>")
    else
        if UI.LiveDot then UI.LiveDot.BackgroundColor3 = T.err end
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.err .. '">no notify remote — use manual box / FORCE SUB</font>')
    end
end)

-- NO Roblox chat listeners (TextChatService / Player.Chatted disabled).
-- Codes + riddles + live memory only come from Sammy's ANNOUNCEMENTS
-- (NotificationController remote → onAnnouncement).
task.defer(function()
    if type(logRich) == "function" then
        logRich('<font color="' .. LOG.dim .. '">chat ignore • only Sammy announcements</font>')
    end
end)

-- FEEDBACK WATCHERS + FOCUS TRACKING
local function watchFeedbackObject(obj)
    if not (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton")) then return end
    if isOurGui(obj) then return end
    handleFeedback(obj.Text or "", obj)
    obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        handleFeedback(obj.Text or "", obj)
    end)
end

for _, obj in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do pcall(watchFeedbackObject, obj) end
playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(watchFeedbackObject, obj)
    end)
end)

UserInputService.TextBoxFocused:Connect(function(box)
    if box:IsDescendantOf(GUI) then return end
    if box ~= currentCodeBox() then return end
    _focused, _lastBox = box, box
    watchBox(box)
end)

UserInputService.TextBoxFocusReleased:Connect(function(box)
    if box:IsDescendantOf(GUI) then return end
    local codeBox = currentCodeBox()
    if box ~= codeBox and box ~= _lastBox then return end
    if cfg.retypeInvalid then
        local submitted = box.Text ~= "" and box.Text or _lastNonBlankText
        rememberPending(box, submitted, false)
    end
    if _focused == box then _focused = nil end
end)

-- CLEANUP HOOK
local function stopEverything()
    if listenConn then
        pcall(function() listenConn:Disconnect() end)
        listenConn = nil
    end
    if viewportConn then pcall(function() viewportConn:Disconnect() end) end
    helperAutoBuyActive = false
    helperAntiRagdollActive = false
    if helperAutoBuyConn then pcall(function() helperAutoBuyConn:Disconnect() end) end
    if helperCharacterConn then pcall(function() helperCharacterConn:Disconnect() end) end
    if helperAntiRagdollConn then pcall(function() helperAntiRagdollConn:Disconnect() end) end
    if HX then
        HX.speed, HX.infJump, HX.noclip, HX.fullbright, HX.antiAfk = false, false, false, false, false
        HX.fly, HX.antiSit = false, false
        for _, key in ipairs({ "speedConn", "infJumpConn", "noclipConn", "fullbrightConn", "antiAfkConn", "flyConn", "antiSitConn" }) do
            if HX[key] then pcall(function() HX[key]:Disconnect() end) HX[key] = nil end
        end
        pcall(function()
            if HX.flyBV then HX.flyBV:Destroy() end
            if HX.flyBG then HX.flyBG:Destroy() end
        end)
        HX.flyBV, HX.flyBG = nil, nil
        pcall(function() setNoclipParts(player.Character, false) end)
        pcall(function() hxApplySpeed(player.Character) end)
    end
    if helperAnchored then pcall(function() setCharacterAnchored(false) end) end
    helperAnchored = false
    clearBoxWatchers()
    if GUI then pcall(function() GUI:Destroy() end) end
end

env.HeadlessRedeemerStop = stopEverything
env.Skyr0Stop = stopEverything
env.StopAura = stopEverything

print("[HEADLESS HUB] code redeemer loaded - deob by Crxkv")


end
local hmOk, hmErr = pcall(HeadlessMain)
if not hmOk then
    warn("[HEADLESS HUB] HeadlessMain error:", hmErr)
    print("[HEADLESS HUB] HeadlessMain error:", hmErr)
    -- Emergency minimal UI so the user is not stuck with nothing
    pcall(function()
        local pg = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        local g = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        g.Name = "HeadlessHub_Fallback"
        g.ResetOnSpawn = false
        g.DisplayOrder = 999999
        g.Parent = pg
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 120)
        f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.2, 0)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 10, 50)
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.Parent = g
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
        c.Parent = f
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16)
        t.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.TextWrapped = true
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        t.TextSize = 13
        t.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 220)
        t.Text = "HEADLESS HUB failed to load UI.\nError: " .. tostring(hmErr) .. "\nCheck F9 console."
        t.Parent = f
    end)
end
